As far as Hyun Jong knew, there was just one person in Mount Hua who could do that.

But Baek Cheon seemed to have other thoughts.

“C-Chung Myung.”

“Uh?”

Baek Cheon’s face, which had been calm until facing Jang Ilso, turned pale.

“W-We have to help.”

“Help?”

“Right! No matter what, we need to help.”

Chung Myung looked at Baek Cheon with an ‘Ah’ face.

“That... still, isn’t it a bit harsh? Still, we need to help them...”

“Huh?”

But Chung Myung opened his eyes wide and asked back as if it was shocking. The reaction was different from what he had expected, so Baek Cheon trailed off and looked at him blankly, while Chung Myung mumbled.

“I knew Dong-Ryong was a bit extreme, but even so, helping the Evil Faction bastards and defeating the Nine Great Sects was a bit... you know? Of course, I know that the resentment is deep, but there are things people can and cannot do.”

“W-What are you talking about, you insane bastard! Of course, we need to help the Nine Sects and defeat the Evil Factions!”

“Eh? Ah, was that what you heard?”

Chung Myung grinned.

“Well. That is a problem too... if a rat and cockroach are fighting in your house, who should you help?”

“...”

“If possible, it’s better for both to lose. Right. And we fight?”

This insane bastard.

It wasn’t like he was insane, which was more of an exclamation; as he often said, he was crazy.

“Of course, guy! Of course, we have to help Evil Factions!”

“Help the Evil Faction!”

“Yah! Justice faction! Justice faction! Damn it! What are you talking about!”

Baek Cheon’s face turned red. Baek Cheon rarely made such kind of talk, but now he was in a situation where he was unable to come to his senses.

“What? Does sasuk like a good guy with a grudge? Or do you like criminals without resentment?”

“Uh? That....”

“See. Because this isn’t an easy problem to handle. Sigh... I am worried about this. Ugh....”

“...”

The saddest thing was that everyone was listening to what Chung Myung was saying.

“Hmm.”

At that time.

Jang Ilso, who had been quietly observing the situation, let out a low snort and opened his mouth.

“Yes. Mount Hua had no reason to help them.”

Chung Myung raised his head and looked at Jang Ilso.

“There might be a reason for Mount Hua to help, but the Heavenly Friends Alliance has no reason to do so. Isn’t that right, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon?”

A smile was clearly visible on the red lips. Chung Myung smiled as he looked at Jang Ilso.

“Yah, Jang Ilso.”

“Speak, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.”

“Shut up.”

“...”

Jang Ilso’s eyes twitched so lightly.

“Looking at that grinning face makes me want to stab a knife between those eyes. Don’t change my mind for no reason, and keep that mouth shut.”

Chung Myung growled and said.

The deadly killing intent he was leaking out reached Jang Ilso.

“...”

Not only the Four Precepts Alliance, but even the sects below the cliff looked at Chung Myung with dumbfounded looks.

Who in the world would even dare say something like that to Jang Ilso? In such a situation!

Chung Myung. No one would be able to do that unless they were Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.

No one else.

Beop Gye realized it right then.

Those who had appeared now were neither saviors nor enemies.

They could offer the hand of salvation, or they could reach out with a blade of ill intent.

In other words... This meant that the fate of not just the sects there, but the entire Kangho depended on the choice that Mount Hua made.

And...

‘Why does it have to be you? Why!’

The person who determined the position of Mount Hua was none other than Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.

At this moment, the fate of the world was rolling in the palms of that mysterious, truly evil person.

‘Why.’

Beop Gye ended up closing his eyes tightly.

The sound of Chung Myung’s laughter could be heard in his ears.

Chapter 808: It Is So Fucking Nice To Meet You Again! (3)

“Let’s see...”

Chung Myung’s gaze turned to the Evil Faction across from him.

“I don’t like those bastards either.”

This time, he looked down the cliff with a look of disapproval.

“I don’t like them either.”

He clicked his tongue with a sour expression on his face.

“Are there no people whom I like in this world? I’m so worried, so concerned.”

At that moment, Yoon Jong, who stood close behind, whispered.

“Yah, Chung Myung.”

“Uh?”

“Are you just saying that? Actually, are you trying to help the Nine Great Sects below?”

Chung Myung looked back at Yoon Jong with an expression of pity.

“No, does this guy have some disease within him? Do you think I’ll help those bastards after such talk? I’d rather feel like pouring oil on them and setting them on fire.”

“...”

“Why? Am I wrong?”

“T-that isn’t it, brat!”

“What?”

Yoon Jong desperately glanced down.

“There, over there!”

“What?”

Since Chung Myung couldn’t understand, Yu Yiseol, who had her eyes closed, and Yoon Jong helped.

“Monk Hae Yeon. There!”

“Eh?”

“Right! Monk Hae Yeon is there! It’s basic for us to save Monk Hae Yeon!”

“Right, sahyung!”

Only then did a look of regret flash over Chung Myung’s face.

“...now that I think about it, he is down there, right?”

He saw Hae Yeon standing in the middle of Shaolin, looking up. The eyes, as big as a cow’s, shining in sadness, seemed impossible to ignore. Chung Myung looked at him and said,

“But...”

“Uh?”

“When a monk dies, they build a pagoda and cremate them, right?”

“...what is this now?”

“No, well...”

Chung Myung scratched the back of his head and smiled.

“It’s going to end like that anyway, so wouldn’t it be unfair to think of setting fire as cremation in advance?”

“What are you talking about, you insane idiot!”

“Shouldn’t you at least speak like a human?”

“Are you even human! Huh?”

In the end, the Five Swords rushed in at once, grabbed Chung Myung by the collar, and shook him. After everyone arrived, it seemed like they didn’t care if anyone saw it, be it Jang Ilso or someone else.

Chung Myung protested that it was unfair to him.

“No! Isn’t Buddhism all about letting go of attachments in life? Saving a life is the biggest obsession anyone could have! In some ways, we could be helping them out, you know?”

“Disgusting bastard! You damned ruined man!”

“You need to be scolded!”

Yoon Jong hit Chung Myung’s mouth with his palm, and Yu Yiseol hit him on the head with her sheath.

“Eh! Don’t be a bother and just let me go! We can do it, as I said! Yah!”

Chung Myung pushed Yu Yiseol and Yoon Jong away as he shouted loudly. Hae Yeon stared at him with wide eyes, not knowing what was even happening.

“You come up!”

“...Uh?”

“You come up now. I don’t think they will die just because you come up, right?”

“...”

“If you just come up, things will be resolved cleanly. Come up fast!”

Hae Yeon smiled.

He knew right away that he was crazy, but...

‘He is crazier than I thought. This is beyond my imagination.’

Chung Myung was now proving with his entire body that an insane person doesn’t care about time or place.

“...How can I go up before the sahyungs?”

“Uh? You won’t come?”

“I cannot come...”

“Ahh, as expected of our bald head, so loyal. Then why don’t you all die together?”

“...”

Clear tears formed around the eyes of Hae Yeon.

‘I couldn’t believe I wanted to see that bastard even for a moment.’

Even after all this...

Meanwhile, Byeok Hyun was speechless and looked at Chung Myung in confusion.

Unruly warrior. Odd language.

“...How can one be so frivolous?”

“There is no such thing other than insane.”

An expression of discomfort formed on the face of Namgung Hwang.

Clearly, the actions of Chung Myung had gone too far. However, two people looked at Chung Myung with different eyes from the rest.

One was Venerable Heo Do, with his somberly sunken eyes.

‘That isn’t a look humans can have just because they want to.’

Humans were animals, so they also felt a sense of crisis. Even the rude man there could not run wild as usual if a sword was at his throat.

But now Chung Myung was acting more violently than he usually does.

‘It probably means that neither Shaolin, Wudang nor even the Ten Thousand People Clan are making that guy nervous.’

He knew. Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon was no idiot who could not understand the situation. Rather, he was excessively fast in calculation and accurately captured the flow of time too.

The self-indulgence of someone who didn’t know the situation was foolish, but wasn’t this the behavior of someone who knew the situation in confidence?

‘Are you confident that you can handle the situation?’

Venerable Heo Do bit his lip.

He had a high evaluation of Chung Myung. Few people in the world valued Chung Myung more highly than him. But at this moment, Venerable Heo Do couldn’t shake off the suspicion that his evaluation of Chung Myung had been entirely off.

And, aside from Venerable Heo Do, another person saw Chung Myung with different eyes.

Jang Ilso slowly opened his mouth.

“You don’t want to keep playing this boring drama, right?”

Chung Myung cast a sharp gaze towards Jang Ilso.

“I thought I told you to keep that mouth of yours shut?”

“I would like to, but you see, if I don’t speak now, this standoff drama will continue forever.”

Chung Myung scratched his head without a word. Even though Jang Ilso said it, it wasn’t entirely wrong.

“Fine. Then go ahead. Instead, be careful of what you say. If you speak nonsense, I will attack without another thought.”

Everyone who heard that was shocked.

Earlier, it was a simple “shut up,” but this time, he said not to speak nonsense. Even the abbot, the leader of Shaolin, wouldn’t treat Jang Ilso that way.

What was more perplexing was that Jang Ilso didn’t seem particularly upset after hearing such harsh words from a young man.

The Desolate Gold Elder, who was silently observing, twisted his mouth and laughed.

“Scammer.”

“Even those words won’t work.”

Jang Ilso nodded and looked at Chung Myung carefully. The Desolate Gold Elder thought his expression seemed a bit cheerful.

‘Is it true that insane people have something in common?’

It was so odd.

If he considered the rank of things in the world, the Desolate Gold Elder would be higher than Jang Ilso. And he couldn’t even dare compare himself to a kid who is supposed to be Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.

The Desolate Gold Elder couldn’t even dare intervene in the talks between the two, though.

These two people had a different kind of power, something unrelated to fame and status, as discussed in Kangho. It couldn’t just be called Kangho; it was something else they didn’t know.

The lips of Jang Ilso turned into a thin smile.

“What about it, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon? The gift I prepared?”

“... What bullshit is this?”

“There is nothing to hide from each other now, right? Surely you want to skin me alive, right?”

“You know me well.”

“But at the same time, you are someone who can cooperate even with the Evil Faction to get what you want.”

“...”

Jang Ilso smiled brightly.

“We create ties by linking clans and sects and bring together those who seem to never come under one roof. This is something that cannot be done unless one has given in to their desires entirely.”

“You seem to be misunderstanding something, but the Heavenly Friends Alliance isn’t like that.”

“No, no, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.”

Jang Ilso shook his head.

“That isn’t important. The important thing is that you have no intention of being satisfied with that level. What you want is for your alliance to grow even bigger! Increasingly! Shocking to devour everything! And more!”

Chung Myung’s eyes narrowed.

“You know me? You speak as if you know me so well?”

“I do know you. I cannot help but know you. Because we are similar.”

Chung Myung’s face contorted.

“Don’t you know that not everything that comes out of a mouth can be taken as a word? I surely said to get lost if you are going to speak such nonsense, right?”

“No, no. For one thing, we are alike.”

Jang Ilso scanned Chung Myung with his eyes.

“Not being satisfied.”

“...”

It wasn’t Chung Myung who flinched at those words but the disciples of Mount Hua who were by his side.

‘He cannot deny it.’

If Jang Ilso was the embodiment of desire, Chung Myung was the embodiment of greed. Didn’t Mount Hua grow quickly thanks to Chung Myung’s greed?

Chung Myung wasn’t satisfied with growth in the short period, and other sects couldn’t even go against him, so he came to the Yangtze River and started another mess.

“So see Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon. Is there a better chance than this?”

“...”

“As long as the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families exist, both the Heavenly Friends Alliance and the Four Precepts Alliance will face limitations. But... if we can take those guys down, the vast land called the Southern Lands can be shared between us two alone!”

Everyone saw Chung Myung as just Chung Myung.

But Jang Ilso was seeing Chung Myung’s ‘position’ clearly. The person he was currently dealing with was Chung Myung, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon, who was the disciple of Mount Hua, but also Chung Myung, the creator of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

“Morality, justice, consultation... surely you realize such worthless words. I am sure you understand how disgusting it is to talk about such things!”

Chung Myung just glared at him without responding. Jang Ilso calmed his voice and smiled.

“Help me, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.”

“...”

“Not a single ant will survive. We won’t let a single person leave here alive. In that case, no one in the world will know what happened. The Four Sects Alliance will take the blame for their deaths. You can just stand and watch from there.”

Jang Ilso lightly spread his arms. His red long sleeves fluttered in the wind, and his accessories jingled.

“Then you can take half control of the world.”

“...”

“It isn’t them who are destroying it. What is collapsing here is the power of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, which have endured for the past 100 years! The world is turning upside down right here! You and I will be changing the world together!”

Jang Ilso’s every word was filled with confidence.

Baek Cheon hesitated and took a step back without realizing it.

It felt like a force, which shouldn’t have reached this point, was pushing him.

‘Jang Ilso...’

The black madness around him covered the cliff.

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes shook.

‘Who... who is this man, and what is he saying?’

The Heavenly Friends Alliance joining hands with the Evil Faction to destroy the Nine Great Sects?

‘What nonsense...’

That could never happen. No, it shouldn’t happen. How can a Justice Faction group and an Evil Faction join forces to attack their own allies?

But, contrary to Venerable Heo Do’s fierce opposition, his reason was constantly whispering that this wasn’t an impossible story.

His trembling eyes looked at Chung Myung.

The core of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families were gathered. If they were to be wiped out here, only the shell of the Nine Great Sects would remain.

Could the Nine Great Sects without Shaolin and Wudang survive? And can the Five Great Families, having lost the Namgung Family and the Sichuan Tang Family, withstand anything?

‘They wouldn’t work.’

No. Even if one could endure it, it was still the same.

Even if the Nine Great sect wins the war, there will be no place for Shaolin, Wudang, Namgung, and Qingcheng.

Regardless of the outcome, depending on the decision of Chung Myung, these 4 sects might eventually vanish from the history of the Central Plains.

‘That cannot happen!’

No matter how many grudges Mount Hua held against them....

At that moment, Venerable Heo Do took a deep breath.

‘Even if he holds a grudge?’

What could he say?

Even if he did have a grudge, did it mean he needed to follow morality? Even though the one who broke the duty that must be maintained first was the one who broke the law?

“...”

At that moment, Venerable Heo Do finally realized how harsh a standard they had been imposing on Mount Hua by calling themselves the Justice faction.

Even though he thought he knew of it, he didn’t know. It was an extremely harsh standard.

So he had no choice but to think.

If he was in the same position, would he protect those who watched their sect burn? To protect their morals?

Was it alright to just take a step back and watch?

An eye for an eye. And all that implied.

Mount Hua would simply be returning what the Nine Great Sects did to them in the past. Then, could they criticize him for making such a choice?

Venerable Heo Do distorted his face and opened his mouth.

“Mount Hua’s Divine...”

But before that, Chung Myung who had been silently listening to Jang Ilso, opened his mouth.

Chapter 809: It Is So Fucking Nice To Meet You Again! (4)

“So...”

Chung Myung said, with his ears perked up.

“Shall we go down there and kill those guys together, then?”

“Yes.”

“If that bothers me, why don’t we just watch what you guys do and eat some soybean soup?”

“Precisely.”

Jang Ilso smiled and nodded. Chung Myung looked back with a puzzled expression.

“You see.”

“Uh?”

Baek Cheon and the Five Swords all gazed at Chung Myung with anxious expressions.

What more was this bastard going to say...

“It does seem like a good offer...”

“Ack! Just shut up!”

“I said not to do it!”

“Please! Please, you bastard, just!”

Looking at Chung Myung’s face, the Five Swords screamed in unison.

“Ah, seriously.”

Chung Myung frowned and turned his head to look at Hyun Jong. Unlike the Five Swords, who reacted violently, he was just looking at Chung Myung with deep eyes.

And those eyes said it.

‘Do whatever you want; Mount Hua will support your decision.’

That was the faith that Hyun Jong showed in Chung Myung.

‘Ehh.’

But Hyun Jong didn’t know.

That look in his eyes filled with firm faith actually made it impossible for Chung Myung to move carelessly.

Chung Myung glanced at the distant sky.

‘Sect leader sahyung.’

If the person standing there was his sahyung, and Chung Myung stood behind him, surely Chung Myung would have taken the chance to kill the damned bastards of the Nine Great Sects.

If his sahyung told him to be quiet, he would yell back, but deep down he would have tried to accept the words of his sahyung.

But the place where Chung Myung was standing now wasn’t behind his sahyung, but the place of his sahyung.

‘This makes people think back.’

Leading a sect was the same as letting go of oneself.

It meant letting go of all the grudges in the heart, the principles of the world, and violent impulses, and living a life solely for Mount Hua.

Only now did he understand how heavy it was.

“Hmm.”

Chung Myung, who was staring at the sky, sighed for a moment before looking at Jang Ilso again.

Jang Ilso watched Chung Myung with deep eyes as if his emotions were difficult to comprehend.

“It seems like you’re at a loss for words. Do I really have to repeat myself two or three times?”

Chung Myung replied with a grin.

“I thought I said I would attack you if you spoke nonsense?”

“...”

Jang Ilso’s face contorted.

“Nonsense?”

“This is why I don’t make deals with Evil Factions.”

Chung Myung stroked his cheek and glanced at Jang Ilso.

“It’s natural that I don’t want to see those Nine Great sects bastards. My anger indeed makes me want to beat the hell out of them right now.”

“Then what’s the problem?”

“But you see, bastard!”

Chung Myung sharply ground his teeth.

“No father kills someone in front of his child! No older brother tells his younger sibling that achieving a goal is worth killing for.”

“...”

“My way of life is mine. It isn’t Mount Hua’s! I have no intention of dictating what path Mount Hua should take. That would be hell.”

“... I agree.”

“Even if I don’t want to, I agree.”

“Ugh, I hate it.”

“...”

When Chung Myung glanced back, the Five Swords cleared their throats and looked away.

Chung Myung looked at them for a moment with trembling eyes, then glared at Jang Ilso even more sharply, anger and contempt in his eyes.

“That is the way people should take. You moron, you don’t even know what Tao is.”

Anyone who led a sect must be an example to those who followed him.

‘If it were sahyung, he would have definitely said it.’

And without a doubt, the most perfect sect leader was his sahyung.

“Duty isn’t something you keep in the hope of returning. Just because everyone doesn’t follow it doesn’t mean you have to follow it. A Taoist is someone who refines himself. The most important thing is whether you can be proud of yourself or not, and not what others think!”

“...”

“It’s better to hold hands with a passing dog than to hold hands with an unhappy person. That’s pitiful, right? Is it not?”

Jang Ilso, who was staring at Chung Myung in silence, slowly opened his mouth.

“Tao?”

“Yes, Tao.”

“Still, I thought maybe I could communicate with you a little, but you began to talk like an idiot. Tao? What does that mean?”

Jang Ilso’s red lips opened up.

“People take refuge in that concept of Tao! Comfort for those who don’t have it! It is just an excuse for someone without courage. Those who hold the world in their hands don’t discuss things like morality. Only those who don’t dare to conform to the world discuss morality!”

Rather than refute those words, Chung Myung nodded slowly.

“Well... that’s right.”

“Um?”

“It isn’t exactly wrong.”

Jang Ilso looked at Chung Myung as if he didn’t understand.

“... How can you reject what I say?”

“You seem to be mistaken, but what I just said needs to be said.”

“...”

“And there is something else I want to say.”

Srrng.

Chung Myung slowly pulled the sword from the sheath. And he aimed the tip at Jang Ilso.

“Just act within your limits, you damn bastard. Anyone who stands by is disgusting. Ignorant people are useless. But... neither the sins of the indifferent nor the sins of the ignorant can reach you.”

A spark lit up inside of Chung Myung’s eyes.

“From the moment the arm of our senior sasuk was cut down by the Ten Thousand People clan, Mount Hua and the Ten Thousand People clan could no longer live under the same sky. I would rather cut off your wrists than join hands with you.”

“Huh...”

Jang Ilso’s lips twitched as he laughed, seemingly incredulous.

“A small being. Are you really going to miss this chance because of a personal grudge?”

“Of course. I am small. In fact, I’m the smallest of the small. So...”

Chung Myung’s eyes were filled with killing intent.

“I will make you clearly feel the resentment this small guy has.”

A sneer appeared on Jang Ilso’s lips.

‘Idiot...’

Even if no one else knew, Jang Ilso understood.

The resentment in Chung Myung’s eyes. And that this resentment wasn’t visible to everyone else.

No, if it was resentment, it might actually run deeper towards those beneath him.

He spoke jokingly as if reciting something predetermined, but even in that short period, what hellish agony must have been going through Chung Myung’s mind.

And yet, the only reason Chung Myung came to this conclusion was.

“A sect... is that really something important?”

To Jang Ilso, the Ten Thousand People Clan was nothing but a tool to achieve his own goals. But, to Chung Myung, he was the tool to build Mount Hua.

If he had intended to use Mount Hua to resolve his resentment, he might have joined hands with Jang Ilso. But Chung Myung set aside his resentment for the sake of his sect.

Even if it meant walking through the flames of hell, he was determined to make Mount Hua a sect that walked the path of justice.

Even though he understood it, he wasn’t convinced. That was a compulsion, close to a bond.

“You pitiful thing.”

Even if he had the courage and position to shake the world with his mind, he was unable to wield the power to his heart’s content.

So pitiful.

“But you are just foolish.”

Jang Ilso looked at Chung Myung with contemptuous eyes and sneered.

“You need to be there to have a sect. The most important thing in the world is me.”

“Ah, right. I had the same thought once.”

Chung Myung’s lips twitched.

“But now I know. There are things in the world that are more important than me, and that is something a fool like you won’t know.”

Something I wouldn’t have known if I hadn’t lost.

I realized it because I lost.

No one, no one in the world, could ever take Mount Hua away from Chung Myung. No matter who it was!

“I don’t get it.”

Jang Ilso clicked his tongue with a bit of an exaggerated look.

He and Chung Myung were clearly similar but also terribly different.

Even Jang Ilso couldn’t figure out where this difference came from.

“That is disgusting.”

“Right. I am disgusted with you too.”

Chung Myung glared at Jang Ilso.

If he had come to Mount Hua or met his sahyung, if he hadn’t known the affection of sahyung and the importance of walking together, he might have turned into someone like Jang Ilso, who grew up in the back alley as an orphan with no place to live.

That’s why he couldn’t look at that even more. This was disgust for Jang Ilso and hatred for him.

‘I found Mount Hua the most frustrating thing in the world.’

For him, Mount Hua was everything and, at the same time, a restraint. It wasn’t once or twice that he felt the chains of Mount Hua were tying him down.

If there had been no Chung Myung, Mount Hua would have been destroyed long ago.

But...

‘Only now.’

The fact that Mount Hua, which was extremely frustrating, did exist caused a constant wailing in his heart.

And disgust for Jang Ilso and hatred for his past self.

‘I was wondering why my stomach twists every time I see that bastard.’

It seemed like it wasn’t just a grudge for the sake of Un Geom.

“So stop talking and attack. I will cut off that hand and give it to you.”

“Hahahaha.”

Jang Ilso laughed out loud like this was fun.

Then, the Desolate Gold Elder laughed next to him.

“It doesn’t seem to be going well?”

“It isn’t really a big deal.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand.

“I don’t know about the Heavenly Friends Alliance, but Mount Hua doesn’t hold much strength. I just wanted to solve it a little easier....”

Jang Ilso’s eyes, looking at Chung Myung and Mount Hua, were now filled with annoyance.

“If you want to turn down the alcohol and drink the punishment, there is nothing you can do. The only way is to twist the neck and shove the alcohol down the throat.”

Jang Ilso lightly gestured towards Mount Hua.

“Kill them all. Along with the ones below. Do not let them interfere.”

“Charge!”

The Ten Thousand People Clan, waiting behind the cliff, shouted and began to run up.

Their direction was Mount Hua.

“Come!”

At the same time, Chung Myung shouted, and the disciples of the Mount Hua Sect all drew their swords.

“Sect leader!”

“Hmm.”

Hyun Jong nodded and raised his voice.

“Defeat the enemies! Mount Hua does not compromise with injustice!”

“Yes!”

Un Am quickly followed and shouted.

“Blow off the wicks where gunpowder is kept! We have to stop them from blowing up the cliff!”

As soon as the words ended, Chung Myung shouted.

“Sasuk! Sago! Sahyung!”

“Yes!”

“Prepare.”

“Is it now?”

“I am already feeling good!”

Chung Myung smiled, revealing his pure white teeth.

He guessed it was good.

Having them behind his back was enough for now.

“At once! Follow me with the determination to die!”

Chung Myung began to run to the edge of the cliff. Baek Cheon, Yu Yiseol, Yoon Jong, and Jo Gul followed behind him as one body.

‘Sect leader sahyung.’

Now he understood.

He might not have been in an upright position either.

Since he was human, too, there was no way he wasn’t swayed by his own desires, and there was no way he wasn’t harassed by his impulses.

And yet, he maintained his path.

Because he had Chung Myung and the other disciples behind him.

And... now, Chung Myung was taking the same path.

“LET’S GOOOOOOO!”

A red plum blossom bloomed like a vision at the tip of Chung Myung’s sword as it rose above the cliff.

Chapter 810: It Is So Fucking Nice To Meet You Again! (5)

A sheer cliff with nothing but rocks was visible, and in that bleak space, red plum blossoms bloomed.

Seeing that amazing sight, the Ten Thousand People Clan let out screams and swung their blades wildly. But it was all futile, like trying to block an incoming wave with a blade.

Swish!

The plum blossom sword qi flew in and cut their bodies with dozens of slashes in a short time. Their bodies, which had stopped breathing even before being cut in half, fell to the ground long after the plum blossom sword had passed through it.

Kwang!

Chung Myung hit the ground with all his might and rushed toward the Ten Thousand People Clan, who hesitated.

A strong advance and terrifying speed.

But Chung Myung’s eyes were as cold as the North Sea ice.

“Tsk!”

The Ten Thousand People Clan was taken aback by the sight of Chung Myung rushing fiercely through the scattered plum blossom sword qi and froze for a moment.

At that moment, the end was decided.

Chung Myung wasn’t one to miss such a chance.

Pang!

The sword flew as if it were tearing through the air and struck off a Ten Thousand People Clan warrior’s head in one swing.

The head, which flew into the air, rolled for a moment and fell as Chung Myung kicked the headless body away.

“Eik!”

The Ten Thousand People Clan warriors were shocked.

Actually, it wasn’t impossible to harm the body of a comrade. If they could hold them, they would do it. If not, they would just hold back the tears and avoid it.

But that was the standard of the Justice Faction, and that was what Chung Myung was aiming for.

“Don’t underestimate me, you brat!”

A Ten Thousand People Clan warrior delivered a slash at the flying corpse of his comrade. The body split in half, splattering blood.

‘How...’

Puak!

But at that moment, the plum blossom sword struck him in the neck.

“Pfft...”

The warrior looked at Chung Myung with red eyes, unable to believe it.

Inside the gap where he had slashed the sword from top to bottom to cut the body.

Chung Myung, who was hiding behind the body, threw himself into the empty space as soon as the sword was lowered.

“...”

They saw the red blood-dotted face of Chung Myung, and their vision began to blur in an instant. The last clear look in Chung Myung’s eyes didn’t hold any emotion or hesitation. The Ten Thousand People Clan understood why they had to die.

Paat!

Chung Myung swung out the sword that had pierced the warrior’s neck and pulled it out, revealing his pure white teeth.

“Youuuuu!”

The Ten Thousand People Clan warrior, who was quite unnerved by the scene, let out a terrifying shout and swung their swords at Chung Myung.

But.

Clang!

The blades were blocked by successive swords before they could reach Chung Myung.

“This...!”

Thud!

Baek Cheon’s sword, which deflected the blades of the enemies, scattered plum blossom sword qi in an instant.

A face that didn’t show even a bit of excitement.

Unlike the swift and flashy sword, Baek Cheon’s face looked cold and sunken. He didn’t seem to have the pride to be called a swordsman.

“Yiseol! Jo Gul!”

“Yes!”

“Take the left side of Chung Myung!”

“Yes!”

“Yoon Jong, follow behind Chung Myung!”

“Yes!”

Baek Cheon’s eyes quickly followed Chung Myung after pushing away the enemy with the plum blossom sword technique.

Bang!

Chung Myung, who stepped down on the ground, rushed ahead again. At that sight, the corners of Baek Cheon’s lips curled up.

This wasn’t an ‘I am going in alone.’

‘I will head first, so catch up without being late.’

Even though he knew that the enemy blades would be flying at him, Chung Myung didn’t look away. Of course, he believed that Baek Cheon would soon come to block the sword.

“There is no way...”

Baek Cheon smiled, showing his teeth. Confidence filled his face.

His sword shone white in the sunlight.

“Do not hesitate! The enemy is the Evil Faction! If you hesitate, you die, and so do our comrades!”

“Yes!”

Following Baek Cheon’s request, a loud reply echoed. The disciples of the Mount Hua Sect charged forward without hesitation.

Kakang!

The first sword slash deflected the incoming spear.

Slash!

The sword swung again and again, cutting down the enemy’s neck.

“Crrrrrr!”

With the strange sound of blood foaming up, the warrior’s body felt rotten. But before the body even touched the ground, Chung Myung’s form was already passing by, rushing to the next enemy.

“This... Euk!”

The Ten Thousand People Clan.

Was there any place in the world that had fought more battles than them?

While other clans were immersed in peace and protecting their own lands, the Ten Thousand People Clan expanded their territory by waging war and killing others in the Central Plains.

Even if they weren’t the strongest people in the world, no one would deny that they were the most familiar with battles.

The War Ghosts of Guangxi. Another name for the Ten Thousand People Clan.

But the Ten Thousand People Clan of today? Were they truly the war ghosts?

They unconsciously winced as they watched Chung Myung run at them without even blinking at the beheaded enemies.

Fear wasn’t something they understood or felt.

But the body reacted before the mind comprehended. A stiffened body slowed down their reaction, and that delay made Chung Myung’s sword seem even faster.

Paat!

A strange smile appeared on Chung Myung’s lips as he cut down another head in a single slash.

“T-this!”

“Shut up!”

Another warrior with a long spear rushed at Chung Myung.

Because Chung Myung charged ahead without looking back, his front, left, and right were surrounded by the warriors of the Ten Thousand People Clan. In battle, being surrounded as a vanguard separated from the main force usually meant death.

The Ten Thousand People clan was the embodiment of battle, having fought a hundred battles. They couldn’t overlook the obvious gap they found in Chung Myung.

“Taat!”

Countless weapons came in: blades, spears, and swords. At that moment, when weapons of different lengths were about to pierce Chung Myung’s body...

“What?”

Chung Myung’s body vanished in an instant as if it had disappeared.

Kakakak!

There was no way to retrieve the weapons that flew almost into his body. Weapons loaded with internal qi collided head-on with one another and bounced back.

“Kuak!”

A strong recoil was felt in their hands. Their internal qi, which had surged through the weapons, began to flow back.

At that moment.

Kwaang!

A terrible pain was felt in the thigh, like a whip tearing through it. The pain continued to spread.

“Ahhhh!”

“Ahhh! My legs! My leggg!”

Chung Myung, who had been lying on the ground to avoid the flying weapons, turned his body and unleashed a circular sword qi. Their moment of weakness came because Chung Myung’s body and the moment of the slash couldn’t be properly identified, obscured by the numerous weapons and the qi surrounding them.

The Ten Thousand People Clan’s warriors, whose legs had been completely severed, fell to the ground, writhing and screaming. Chung Myung rushed ahead like a snake, keeping as low as possible.

Slash! Slash!

Chung Myung’s sword, which struck down the fallen like a snake, moved without stopping. He neatly cut off the hands of those who had fallen and rushed ahead, leaving the rest behind.

“K-kill you...!”

The movements were extremely practical, yet also bizarre. There wasn’t a hint of hesitation when it came to killing someone. The cruelty of cutting the heads off those who had lost their legs and were unable to fight would make even the worst sick.

“That bastard must die!”

With someone’s shout, the morale of the Ten Thousand People Clan rose again. But that wasn’t a decision one could make lightly.

Kwang!

Chung Myung hit the ground and launched himself like a predator.

Paaat!

The Plum Blossom sword technique requires the right distance.

There were usually ways to create distance. One was to retreat, and the other was to force the enemy to retreat.

Flowers of red bloomed before the eyes of those hesitating, unable to rush forward due to Chung Myung’s strength.

“ACKKKKK!”

“Kuaak!”

The Plum Blossom sword qi enveloped the Ten Thousand People clan at the front.

Even the screams seemed a luxury to those standing ahead. They became mere lumps of blood, dying without even a chance to shout.

In the moment they faced death, they would have understood one thing for sure.

The price of their pride for widening the distance against Mount Hua’s Swordsman.

Thud!

Tak!

As those who had stopped breathing fell, Chung Myung stepped onto the ground.

Chung Myung’s gaze, which had been coldly fixed on the Ten Thousand People clan, slowly turned to the side.

At that moment.

Jang Ilso’s eyes, staring at the opposite cliff, and Chung Myung’s eyes, which had overcome blood and death, met in the air.

The Desolate Gold Elder shook his head with a look of disgust.

“...That isn’t a human.”

“Why is that guy there?”

“Horrible.”

Even the Thousand Face Scholar and Black Dragon King couldn’t take their eyes off Chung Myung.

Was he strong?

It was actually a good thing. Because there were many strong people.

But such venom was something even those who had overcome tough battles had never seen before.

“...That thing must be killed.”

The moment those words came out of Desolate Gold Elder’s mouth, Jang Ilso clicked his tongue and licked his lips. The tongue grazing and wrapping around the red lips was like a snake.

“What are you doing?”

“Uh?”

“Light it up.”

Everyone was captivated by Chung Myung, but Jang Ilso was the exception.

“Do not be distracted by that clown’s actions. Well, he cannot make it this far. Blow up the cliff and bury those below!”

“Yes!”

Those who came forward with a lantern moved to light the installed wick.

Grumble!

The oil-soaked cloth caught flame in an instant, and in a moment, dozens of flames began to slide down the cliff like red snakes.

At that moment.

Tat!

Chung Myung kicked the ground and propelled himself upward.

The Ten Thousand People Clan warriors were scared and flinched, but the direction Chung Myung flew wasn’t towards them.

“What?”

The warriors were shocked, their mouths agape.

Chung Myung suddenly jumped down the cliff.

“What is he doing?”

Those at the edge of the cliff urgently looked down.

And at that moment, they saw.

Crack! Crack!

The cliffs. Chung Myung was running down the side of the steep cliff that had seemed impossible to climb.

Shock flashed through their wide-open eyes.

“That... that...”

Tatatak!

Chung Myung ran in a straight line towards the burning wicks. It looked as if he was running on flat ground.

In one hand, he held the Dark Fragrance Plum Blossom Sword.

A bitter chuckle on his lips.

The sight would never be erased from the minds of not just the Ten Thousand People Clan warriors but every warrior present.

“Stop him, you bastards! What are you all watching for!”

At that moment, for the first time, a loud shout came out of Jang Ilso’s mouth. Startled, the Ten Thousand People Clan warriors gritted their teeth and threw themselves down the cliff too.

And Jang Ilso’s shouting awakened not only the Ten Thousand People Clan.

“Jump! Right now! Put out the wick!”

“Yes!”

Shaolin and Wudang, who had momentarily been distracted by the fierce battle happening on the cliff, hurriedly began to run.

“Damn it! Don’t fall behind! The only way to live is to move up!”

And after that, Namgung and Qingcheng also began to climb the cliff with all their might.

Live to kill.

Those who wanted to kill and those who wanted to survive.

At the mouth of the flowing Yangtze River, a fierce battle between Justice and Evil began to unfold for the first time in 100 years.

Chapter 811: I Will Handle It All, You Bastard! (1)

Chung Myung, who was running along the cliff as if it were flat ground, suddenly raised his gaze to the top of the cliff.

The Ten Thousand People Clan, who were occupying the space above, were descending at high speed to stop him. The warriors pouring out of the basin one after another began to show hostility.

“DIEEEE!”

About a dozen Ten Thousand People Clan members came swinging their weapons and throwing themselves at Chung Myung. They didn’t show even the slightest care for their lives. Their only intent was to cut Chung Myung down or have him fall to his death!

“Huh!”

Chung Myung snorted briefly and bared his teeth.

Paaa!

His sword split the air in a beam of light. In an instant, dozens of sword qi emerged, slicing through the Ten Thousand People Clan warriors descending the cliff.

A sword must adapt to the situation.

For those falling at a speed they couldn’t match, there was no need for complexity. All they needed was speed! It was so fast that it couldn’t be avoided.

Slash! Slash! Slash!

With an eerie sound, the red sword qi pierced the bodies of the Ten Thousand People Clan.

“Kuak!”

But it wasn’t a situation where they could simply give up and suffer.

Even as they were pierced by the sword qi and bled, their eyes remained focused on Chung Myung.

Eyes filled with poison.

Both arms spread wide.

The Ten Thousand People Clan kicked off the cliff and rushed at Chung Myung.

“Chung Myungggg!”

Baek Cheon watched the scene while fighting on the cliff, screaming.

The enemy’s intention to hold onto Chung Myung and fall together, even if it meant losing their lives, was palpable to him.

If this place wasn’t a cliff, just keeping a distance to avoid being touched would suffice. However, no matter how strong Chung Myung was, avoiding such an attack would be difficult in that terrain.

“N-No...!”

Just as Baek Cheon was thinking it might have been a mistake for Chung Myung to jump down.

Pat!

Chung Myung kicked off the cliff and leaped to avoid the oncoming Ten Thousand People Clan.

“What?”

A gasp escaped from both Baek Cheon and Jo Gul simultaneously.

Chung Myung’s body soared into the air.

“Damn!”

“Hey, you idiot!”

Mount Hua’s disciples widened their eyes. No matter how skilled Chung Myung was, he couldn’t survive a fall from that height. The distance was too great to adjust his position mid-air.

“Eik! This crazy guy!”

But then, Chung Myung flipped over in the air, descended swiftly, and shouted.

“Now!”

Woooong!

At that moment, a golden qi rose from beneath the cliff.

“Hahaha!”

Chung Myung laughed briefly, stepped on the qi rising beneath him, and propelled himself back to the cliff.

“Hae Yeon!”

“Monkkkkk!”

The disciples of Mount Hua cheered in amazement.

A person who could execute a fist technique from such a distance and do it gently enough not to harm the one stepping on it.

Was there anyone else like Hae Yeon?

“Insane bastards!”

Baek Cheon’s face was filled with joy as he cursed.

Chung Myung, who trusted that Hae Yeon below would handle it, launched himself into the air without hesitation. At the same time, Hae Yeon, anticipating Chung Myung’s jump, was ready to strike.

Even though they hadn’t exchanged a single word, they knew what each other was going to do.

“Damn it! I feel so jealous!”

Jo Gul giggled and shouted.

“Sasuk!”

“I know!”

Baek Cheon glanced back. Hyun Sang, who made eye contact with him, nodded his head.

“Push forward! Do not let those people target Chung Myung! We need to protect him!”

“Yes!”

The Mount Hua sect, who had been hesitating, gained courage and rushed forward to confront the Ten Thousand People Clan on the cliff.

“Tsk!”

“You little bastards!”

The Ten Thousand People Clan was already taken aback by the heightened momentum of Mount Hua.

This cliff wasn’t structured to allow many people to move. Even if their strength was amazing, the number of people who could fight against Mount Hua was bound to be limited.

In the meantime, there was a need to bring in people who could keep Chung Myung in check at the bottom, so a vacuum was bound to occur.

Moreover, morale was clearly higher in Mount Hua.

“What are you doing?”

Then, a low voice came.

“Military head!”

Ho Ga-Myung, who had been watching, asked with his cold, glaring eyes.

“Right, see that, kids. Push them out clearly. Remember, the goal isn’t to defeat them but to block them.”

“Yes!”

The troops once again grabbed their weapons and clenched them. Ho Ga-Myung glanced down the cliff.

‘No way?’

Did he calculate all this before going down the cliff?

That daredevil?

Ho Ga-Myung felt goosebumps rise on his body and bit his lip.

Tatatatatad!

Chung Myung, who tore off the terrifyingly clinging warriors and ran up the cliff, rushed to the first burning cloth wick.

Tat!

And in one go, the lower part was cut down with the sword.

Kang!

“Oh?”

Chung Myung’s eyes shone.

His sword slash was enough to leave a strong scar on the cliff, but the cloth stuck to it wasn’t completely cut.

“This bastard!”

Chung Myung let out this roar again. When he cut it off in one slash, the lower part fell off the cliff and hung down.

Chung Myung glared at Jang Ilso on the cliff across from him. He must have thought in advance that one would try to cut the cloth by using the sword qi and used a special cloth as a wick.

It wasn’t usually possible to pay much attention to small things when planning for a huge event.

“Jump!”

But there was no time to think. The Ten Thousand People Clan rushed down the cliff and moved toward Chung Myung.

Their level of arts seemed higher than those he dealt with before.

“Huh!”

But Chung Myung only greeted them with a snort.

Pat!

He climbed up the cliff and rushed toward those running to him.

Sheer cliffs loomed overhead as Chung Myung’s sword met the downward slashes of the Ten Thousand People warriors.

The warriors’ eyes gleamed with confidence.

‘What a foolish move!’

They descended from above while he leaped from below. Their weapon was a heavy blade, whereas he wielded a thin sword.

If they clashed directly, it was clear who would be overpowered!

‘I’ll bring you down, you arrogant fool!’

But at the moment their weapons met in mid-air,

Kakaka!

Chung Myung’s sword twisted slightly, and then he pushed it upwards from below.

“Uh?”

Their bodies were nudged upwards. It was subtle, but enough. Normally, they could regain balance by simply lowering their foot.

But here, on the cliff, a body thrown off balance would plummet.

“Ah, this damned...”

Chung Myung soared past the warriors, struggling to stabilize themselves. Without looking back, he ascended with the determination of a snake climbing a cliff, diving into another group of Ten Thousand People clan troops charging in.

Taaak!

Minimum strength, maximum effect.

This movement differed from before, where a single sword strike would unbalance the opponent. Now, Chung Myung’s sword was full of grace. He avoided harming them, instead using minimal force to send them off the cliff.

And, of course, the outcome was anything but gentle.

“You bastard!”

“Damn it!”

The Ten Thousand People clan members, falling from the cliff, panicked and redirected their qi.

After several attempts, they managed to cling to the cliff again, but the situation they faced was far from favorable.

“Hit them!”

“These Evil Faction bastards!”

The Wudang disciples, who were climbing up, saw the enemies, drew their swords, and kicked them off the cliff.

“This!”

In the middle of the cliff, the Ten Thousand People Clan, Wudang, and Shaolin were clashing weapons against one another.

“Ahhh!”

The result was decisive.

No matter how strong the Ten Thousand People Clan was and despite occupying the upper part, only a dozen people were able to deal with Wudang, who were pushing up.

The Ten Thousand People Clan, instantly pierced by swords and turned into hedgehogs, fell down the cliff.

But.

Suddenly.

“W-what...”

“Kuak... ahh... damn... Justice Faction... bastards.”

Even though their bodies were pierced, the remaining enemies grabbed the Wudang disciples, clinging to the cliff. They grinned with shining eyes.

“Let’s go together.”

Crack!

“ACKKKK!”

One of Wudang’s disciples screamed and fell. The cliff he was holding onto until the very end broke apart, and the stone dust fell down with him.

“Jin MYUNGGGGGG! Elder! Jin Myung is!”

“Ignore it and climb up!”

“Elder!”

“Damn it! Don’t you know? If you stay down, you will die anyway! If you want to save Jin Myung, cut the bloody wick! Up! Move up!”

“Damn it...!”

Chung Myung smiled at that sight.

“I cannot be the only one suffering.”

He mumbled and jumped up the cliff.

“Baldie!”

“OHHHH!”

Hae Yeon, waiting below, immediately used a fist qi.

As the Wudang said a moment back, their lives could not be guaranteed if they remained below. The path they came on was a path of death. There was no way that Jang Ilso would have left that path open for them.

To survive, they needed to desperately climb up and prevent the cliff from collapsing.

But Hae Yeon stayed below without doubt or wavering.

“Taoist!”

“Okay!”

Chung Myung stepped on the fist qi and jumped to the other side of the cliff.

Kakakakng!

Chung Myung, who cut off three wicks at once, glanced at Jang Ilso.

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso’s face was full of interest as he watched the situation unfold.

“Hegemony Lord. No, Alliance Lord. Maybe the situation wasn’t so easy?”

Jang Ilso laughed softly at the Desolate Gold elder’s words.

“Hmm.”

A strange light shone in his eyes.

“I guess we did underestimate him? Then we need to give a gift.”

Snap!

Jang Ilso snapped his fingers. A moment later, something flew out. Desolate Gold Elder, who confirmed the identity of what the Ten Thousand People Clan was moving, stayed silent for a moment and said,

“... you prepared in advance?”

“Of course.”

“...”

Color had drained from his face.

‘Since when did you start to orchestrate the situation?’

At least before they met. So, does it mean that Jang Ilso had the confidence that everyone in the Shenzhou Five Clans, except the Green Forest, would be under his command? Before he even met them?

“Precious people are coming over to meet us, so of course I had to prepare a gift.”

Jang Ilso looked down with eyes full of joy.

Venerable Heo Do, who was climbing the cliff in front, stopped in shock when he noticed the look on Jang Ilso’s face.

As if in response to his premonition, after a while, black cauldron-like things appeared on the cliff.

‘T-that... that is?’

Jang Ilso whistled and spoke cheerfully.

“Pour it.”

A scream came, tearing from the mouth of Venerable Heo Do.

“Avoid it! No, stop! It is oil! OIL! Boiling oil is being poured down!”

Dozens of pots tilted at once, and boiling oil began to pour down on their heads.

The eyes of the Nine Great Sects disciples who were climbing the cliff were filled with deep fear and despair.

Chapter 812: I Will Handle It All, You Bastard! (2)

It was an overwhelming sight.

As boiling oil poured from dozens of pots larger than a person, it looked like a waterfall cascading down for an instant.

But what was now pouring onto their heads wasn’t a cold waterfall but boiling oil that would melt flesh in a moment.

A harsh shout came from Venerable Heo Do.

“Stop them! Protect the disciples!”

There was nothing more to do.

The elders of Wudang, who were keeping pace with the disciples climbing the cliff, led by Venerable Heo Do, quickly ascended the cliff. One step ahead of them, the elders of Shaolin did the same.

He realized that if the oil fell on the disciples, the casualties would be enormous.

“Ohhh!”

It was the Shaolin who flew in first.

Their golden fists and kicks raced over the cliff. Dozens of streams of qi attacks at once pushed away the oil.

Paat!

The lumps of oil, which fell together in all directions, moved as if hit by a windshield.

“Tsk!”

It could be destroyed.

It could be cut.

But water and oil were impossible to block entirely.

The oil, which had splattered in all directions, now fell in small drops like rain.

“Don’t stop! Break it! Push it out!”

Beop Gye shouted with all his might.

The blood vessel standing out from his neck showed how anxious he was. And the attacks of Shaolin continued one after another.

It was an amazing sight, but no matter how great the strength, it couldn’t stop the pouring oil rain.

The people who blocked the oil drops, which fell past the elders of Shaolin, were none other than Wudang.

“Spread out!”

With the shout of Venerable Heo Do, the elders of Wudang stabbed the air numerous times. In an instant, more than a dozen sword qi and sword forms appeared, and over a hundred more appeared above them.

The sword forms came into close contact and increased more and more.

Sword Shield.

A sword screen-like defense by Wudang elders covered Shaolin and Wudang disciples like a roof.

Tap. Tap. Tap.

With the sound of water hitting a tent, the oil struck the shield and bounced off.

It was a scene reminiscent of a person blocking the falling rain. It also demonstrated the greatness of the two sects, Shaolin and Wudang, which had reached the highest level.

“Ohh.”

Jang Ilso’s eyes gleamed with amusement.

“Their reputation isn’t for nothing. Maybe because they are Shaolin and Wudang?”

A smile formed on his lips.

“But... how long can you stop it?”

Jang Ilso clicked his fingers again, and the empty pots were thrown to the back while new pots were brought in.

“Pour it.”

The pots, which looked larger than people, tilted again, and new oil began to fall.

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes contorted at that.

‘How many times...?’

If it had been poured all at once, they could handle it. But if he continued to pour it like this, the consumption of internal qi would be extreme.

No matter how great an elder of Shaolin was, they could not continue to display the same level of strength as they did at the start. Even the elders of Wudang could not maintain the same level of sword qi.

Venerable Heo Do gritted his teeth as he felt his internal qi slowly draining.

‘What does this mean then?’

He hadn’t had the chance to interact with the Ten Thousand People Clan or the others.

Were they not warriors?

Had they not learned martial arts?

But in a war between the skilled, they were using explosives to blow up the cliff and pouring boiling oil?

This was different from the war they had expected.

“Pour it again!”

Following the order of Jang Ilso, pots appeared again.

Chakkk!

And the poured oil continued to hit the sword and splash. It was blown by the wind or thrown into the back.

“Ahhh!”

“Mu Bo! Are you alright?”

“Kuak..., my back....”

White steam rose from the oil-splattered disciple. The foul smell of flesh being cooked pierced the nose. Those who saw it felt a chill run down their spines.

But even first aid was impossible while they were hanging on the cliff.

“Eik!”

The disciples of Wudang, driven by courage, climbed the cliff. If they waited, their losses would only accumulate further.

But then.

“Tsk tsk. It’s because you are so impatient.”

Jang Ilso clicked his tongue with an exaggerated expression and laughed. His white hand waved, indicating something again.

“When people are scared, they don’t see the obvious things. This is why experience is important.”

And at that moment.

Kiing.

Venerable Heo Do was startled by the eerie sound that reached his ears and looked down.

‘What?’

At that moment, he saw it.

“N-No!”

The Black Dragon Pirates had suffered extensive damage from the falling rocks, but that didn’t mean they were all dead.

The surviving pirates were now aiming their bows at the cliff below.

Chik!

As soon as the strings were taut and released, arrows began to rise and fly toward the cliff.

“The arrows are coming!”

“Stop them!”

The disciples of Wudang hurriedly swung their swords to strike them down, and the Shaolin monks used their qi to push them aside.

But they couldn’t block all the arrows while clinging to the cliff.

Puak! Puak! Puak!

“Kuaaak!”

A Shaolin monk was struck by an arrow in the back and began to fall.

“Gak So!”

Those around him reached out their hands and caught him. But because he had to endure the arm being struck by an arrow, it was hard.

Crack!

“Kuaak!”

The monk’s face twisted in pain as he grabbed his companion with the hand that had an arrow stuck in the bone.

“Damn it, Qingcheng...!”

Venerable Heo Do gritted his teeth.

If they had climbed the cliff first, the sects below should have cleared the remaining remnants. That was expected.

But how could that be done? Everyone knew that the bottom of the cliff was a dead spot. If the cliff collapsed while they were slaying the pirates, they would be trapped.

The disciples of Qingcheng had no other choice but to climb the cliff.

Taking the risk of dying to save someone else was never easy.

The reason why Buddhism was revered was because few people practiced it. This meant that it was a matter of praising those who did it, not criticizing those who didn’t follow it.

‘Jang Ilso!’

Where did those arrows and bows even come from?

It was clear that this picture was drawn in the mind of Jang Ilso right from the start.

To make matters worse, the oil, which the Sword Shield was unable to block, began to fall down. It cooled while flowing down the cliff and didn’t burn the hands, but the problem was that the bottom of the cliff was now as slippery as ice.

And the arrows from behind were a huge pain in the rear.

To prevent that, they had to remove the gunpowder from the cliff.

There was no such thing as hell. This was hell itself.

Gunpowder collapsed the cliff’s face.

Boiling oil poured over heads.

Arrows flying from below.

Each one of these might not be great alone. But when each was used in the right manner, it turned into a hellish trap from which no one could escape.

“Jang Ilsoooo!”

Came the scream from Venerable Heo Do’s mouth.

“This, now.”

Jang Ilso laughed, wiping the corner of his eyes, and looked down at Venerable Heo Do.

“Oh my, the sect leader of Wudang turned out to be quite impatient! The sect leader’s burden must be heavy.”

“That also depends on the situation. If I were in a situation like that, I would curse without end.”

The Thousand Faced Scholar stuck his tongue out.

The art of strategy was essentially half and half.

The most important thing was how good a position one could get and how well one could use the right strategy from that position. Strategies that changed common things and would remain in history were only used in small wars. Real warfare depended on how perfectly one implemented what was already known.

And the textbook answer was right here.

Who in the world would have thought that arrows, oil, and gunpowder could fool Shaolin and Wudang?

In that formidable plan, Black Dragon King, Thousand Face Scholar, and Desolate Gold Elder were just pieces on a chessboard.

At least here, Jang Ilso and his forces were holding the world in their hands and shaking it up as they pleased.

‘It’s surprising that I don’t feel bad, though.’

The Evil Faction consisted of people who pathologically disliked being influenced by others. Both the Desolate Gold Elder and Black Dragon King surely knew they were being fooled by Jang Ilso’s plan.

But their faces didn’t show hostility or anger.

‘Perhaps... the Four Precepts Alliance might not really be a place of simple talk.’

At that moment, Jang Ilso was proving to everyone that he was qualified to be the Alliance leader.

And there was just one person who slipped from Jang Ilso’s grasp.

“No! YOU FUCKING BASTARD!”

Kakakakak!

Chung Myung screamed as he cut off the three wicks at once.

“Will you not do it quickly? Do you have a grudge against Shaolin too?”

As if in response to those words, the strength that burst out immediately attacked the Black Dragon pirates who were firing arrows. The pirates flew away like leaves.

“Hae Yeon!”

Came the shout from Beop Gye.

While everyone was climbing the cliff to save themselves, he stood below with confidence.

The contorted eyes of Beop Gye looked at Hae Yeon, and he noticed the gentle look on the disciple’s face.

“Amitabha. Elder, please head up. Leave the bottom to....”

“Ah, if you have time to talk, fight well, you bastard!”

“Ahoo! That bastard... ahh. Amitabha! Amitabha!”

Hae Yeon, who frowned for a moment, was startled and continued to chant. He rushed at the pirates as if he had nothing more to say.

The pirates holding the bows couldn’t fight properly and were swept away.

‘Did it!’

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes shone again.

Of course, the numbers below are so high that one person cannot handle them all at once.

But just drawing their attention is effective. Hasn’t the momentum of arrows flying to the cliff already decreased?

‘A bit more...’

“AHOOO! So fucking frustrating that I might die! What are you looking at?”

At that moment, Chung Myung, who was clinging to the cliff, shouted.

“Leave the disciples behind and jump up first! Be it Shaolin, Wudang, or Namgung! I have to fucking feed things into your mouth!”

“That damned bastard!”

The first person to respond to those words was none other than Namgung Hwang.

“Fine! Here I come!”

He soared onto the cliff in one jump. Namgung Hwang, using the Wudang disciples, moved and took the help of Venerable Heo Do’s waist, rising up like a dragon ascending a cliff.

“Tsk!”

Venerable Heo Do gritted his teeth.

Chung Myung’s words were right. If they just blocked it there, they would lose strength and die. Even if they did take damage, they had to occupy the top of the cliff.

“Those who can follow me, follow!”

“Yes!”

Some of the elders retrieved their swords and joined Venerable Heo Do.

“OHH!”

Venerable Heo kicked off the cliff with an odd spirit. And he soared up and up.

“You should have done this a long time ago, morons!”

Pat!

Chung Myung ran up the cliff. The place where he was moving to was the place Jang Ilso was standing.

“I am pointing right at it, you bastard!”

Chung Myung’s loud voice spread over the cliff and into the sky.

Chapter 813: I Will Handle It All, You Bastard! (3)

Oil rained from the sky, and arrows shot out endlessly from the ground.

The qi from Shaolin monks moving up and down the cliff was like a golden dragon, and the Sword Shield drawn by Wudang covered the cliff in white.

‘What is this?’

Bake Cheon’s eyes trembled.

Even though they were in the middle of a battle, he had to calm his mind. Yet, anyone who witnessed this scene would understand why Baek Cheon was distracted.

Not just Baek Cheon and Mount Hua’s disciples, but even the warriors of the Ten Thousand People Clan who were facing him couldn’t take their eyes off it.

A sight that made “overwhelming” feel like an understatement.

Strength and stratagem.

Justice and Evil.

Everything that constituted the battlefield of Kangho clashed fiercely there. As was said in Kangho, there was no law or agreement there.

The only thing that existed was a terrible intent to kill another person and a strong desire to survive at all costs.

The smell of boiling oil and gunpowder pierced the nose, adding to a thick stench that defied explanation.

‘This is a battle...!’

It felt like something sticky and heavy was in his body. He couldn’t breathe properly and felt dazed the entire time as if a fog clouded his mind.

Baek Cheon, who had followed Chung Myung to various battles, knew that his skills might be first-rate, but his experience wasn’t.

But this was different from any battlefield he had experienced.

The scale of the clashing forces was different, and the will behind the conflict was different. Above all, the level of malevolence directed at each other was on a different level.

“Go up! Climb!”

Shaolin and Wudang disciples, with their eyes red and gritted teeth, climbed up. Even as the boiling oil touched and grazed their skin, they continued to move upward.

And the Evil Faction members, who saw that scene, began to pour more oil.

No, it wasn’t just oil anymore.

Seeing the disciples rise higher and higher, they grew impatient. They threw rocks without even being ordered, pouring all their qi into the pile of stones they moved.

Puaak!

The stone that the sword couldn’t completely cut struck a monk who was climbing the cliff.

His head was injured, with blood flowing down. The monk, who stumbled once, gritted his teeth and glared up.

“You... demons!”

His face was close to a demon’s. He grabbed a stone sticking out of the cliff and climbed up. There was no mercy or gentleness in his face anymore. It was just filled with malice towards the other person.

‘This is a real battle!’

When people’s lives were at stake, their true nature came out.

This battlefield was a place where human nature was shown for what it was. And here, there was no sign of the degree or agreement they had been shouting loudly about.

‘No, no!’

Not yet. This could not be the true battlefield of warriors.

“Sasuk!”

“Right.”

Yoon Jong seemed to feel that way, too, and shouted in an angry tone.

How many times had they not seen this far? This was just the start. When the true battle of warriors began...

“They are moving!”

With Jo Gul’s shout, Namgung Hwang and Venerable Heo Do began to climb up the cliff at a blinding speed. A step behind, Beop Gye also led the elders of Shaolin and jumped up the cliff like a dragon ascending to heaven.

Baek Cheon looked far above and searched for one person with his eyes.

“I will handle it all! You bastards!”

With a loud voice, Chung Myung rushed down the cliff like a snake. He was finally making his move.

‘This is it!’

Baek Cheon gritted his teeth.

Until then, it had been closer to a war than a battlefield of warriors. However, this was the most decisive factor that differentiated both.

An absolute warrior.

Absolute warriors who could turn the battlefield’s flow with a single sword or change the land with a single slash.

Their movements determined the direction of the battle.

“Chung Myung!”

Baek Cheon watched Chung Myung sprint along the cliff, while the Ten Thousand People Clan aimed for him from below.

As soon as he saw this, Baek Cheon’s mouth moved before his mind could catch up.

“Sasuk! Senior Sasuk!”

“Yes!”

“We’ll handle it here!”

Before Un Geom and Hyun Sang could fully respond, Baek Cheon swung his sword sharply at the Ten Thousand People Clan in front of him and roared.

“Yiseol! Yoon Jong! Jo Gul!”

“Yes.”

“Yes, Sasuk!”

“I’m ready!”

“M-Me!”

Baek Cheon responded to Tang Soso’s urgent call without delay.

“Soso, support our warriors! We can’t let a single person die!”

“Yes!”

“Let’s go! Chung Myung must be protected!”

“Yes!”

Without hesitation, Baek Cheon and four others leaped down the cliff.

Baek Cheon descended a few steps and touched his toes against the cliff.

Chikkk!

The moment his speed slowed down a bit!

Tat!

Baek Cheon kicked off the cliff, rushing ahead at lightning speed.

“S-Sahyung!”

“You bastards, this is what Mount Hua is!”

At the sight of the Five Swords running in perfect order, cheers erupted one after another from the Mount Hua disciples.

According to the natural order, many people could be superior to them. But when it came to navigating a cliff, everything changed. Which sect in the world would train to walk on a cliff?

“This cliff is nothing compared to Mount Hua!”

Baek Cheon soon ran past the slippery cliff as if it were flat land, chasing after Chung Myung.

He saw the warriors of the Ten Thousand People Clan moving towards Chung Myung, who was already engaged with another person. It was too late for him to intervene directly.

But there was nothing to worry about. Because there was one person here who possessed footwork techniques incomparable to his own.

“Samae!”

“Yes.”

At that moment, Yu Yiseol, who was running behind Baek Cheon, climbed up the cliff. Baek Cheon maintained his running speed and held the sword ahead.

Yu Yiseol rotated her body and fell onto Baek Cheon’s sword. The moment her foot touched the surface of the sword, Baek Cheon swung the sword at once and quickly propelled Yu Yiseol’s body.

Swish!

The surging Yu Yiseol’s body moved like a bird flying off the cliff.

“Die!”

Meanwhile, the Ten Thousand People Clan warriors, who had almost reached Chung Myung, swung their blades with all their might. They were determined to somehow attack Chung Myung and make him fall!

But, rather than even turning to look at them, Chung Myung only rushed ahead.

“You!”

It was the moment when feeling ignored, they pushed even harder into the province.

Paaat!

Yu Yiseol flew in at once and spun around with her sword unleashed. The bright red sword qi that erupted from the tip of the sword swept away the warriors aiming for Chung Myung in an instant.

“Oh!”

The sword qi, which suddenly flew in, frightened the Ten Thousand People Clan and slashed at their bodies. One person did succeed in blocking the flying sword qi with a hastily raised sword, but Chung Myung had already run far ahead of them.

“This woman!”

The eyes of the enemies were all red as they swung their swords at Yu Yiseol. Still, after hitting the cliff a couple of times, she instantly rose above their heads and shot forward again.

“W-what?”

The Ten Thousand People Clan were all wide-eyed.

‘On this cliff?’

Kicking off the ground and jumping over the heads of people wasn’t a big deal. It was acceptable to admit that she moved at a speed invisible to the naked eye. Still, in any case, it would be more difficult to find someone among those gathered here who couldn’t show such a level of footwork.

But wasn’t this place on a cliff?

Kakakakang!

Falling without any gain, they urgently drove a blade into the cliff. Somehow, they succeeded in standing up, but by that time, Chung Myung and Yu Yiseol had already distanced themselves.

“Cha-chase...”

“Who are you chasing, you damn bastard!”

At that moment, Yoon Jong and Jo Gul, who had followed them from the cliff, rushed at them with frightening momentum.

The Ten Thousand People Clan warriors was shocked.

‘No, NO....’

Slash!

There was no way they could properly respond to the attack coming from behind, as they barely managed to stay upright by sticking their swords into the cliff. Those in the front were instantly pierced and fell to the ground, splashing blood, while those in the back gave up fighting and threw themselves down.

Yoon Jong and Jo Gul, who had cleared out a dozen warriors in an instant, had already passed by and shouted at Baek Cheon, who was running ahead.

“I will follow right after you!”

“Okay!”

Baek Cheon gritted his teeth and continued to run. In his eyes, the image of Chung Myung running up the cliff and Yu Yiseol running on top of it as if guarding Chung Myung came into view.

“Don’t let Chung Myung stop!”

As if in response to the shouts, power surged from below and flew straight to the top of Chung Myung’s head.

Hae Yeon was also perfectly assisting in moving.

“T-that...”

The disciples of the Nine Great Sects, who gritted their teeth, climbed the cliff and couldn’t hide their astonishment at the sight.

They had heard of the reputation of Mount Hua’s Five Swords. However, they thought they were only second and third-class disciples.

It was ridiculous that these people could just run along the cliff as if it was flat and easily jump over the rocks like flying squirrels, but to think they did such tricks on the cliff?

How was it possible unless they had ten lives?

Jin Hyun gritted his teeth.

‘Damn it, Righteous Sword of Hua...!’

When he first saw them in the Martial Arts Tournament, the difference between him and Baek Cheon wasn’t so significant.

But now, he couldn’t even dream of imitating the way Baek Cheon was moving. At this moment, he was barely crawling up the cliff, while Baek Cheon was soaring like a bird.

‘What could possibly cause such a difference?’

His eyes were red with frustration.

Yet the anger he felt was nothing compared to what Namgung Do-wei was experiencing.

“This...”

Namgung Do-wei, watching Chung Myung from below, felt his blood boiling, unaware that his clenched lips were bleeding.

“Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon!”

Chung Myung was truly clinging to the cliff like a Divine Dragon. But instead of splitting the mountain with his Great Sword, he was struggling and groaning?

“Ugh!”

In a fit of rage, Namgung Do-wei began climbing the cliff with intense movements.

“Do-wei, calm down!”

“It is dangerous!”

The elders of the family shouted urgently, but Namgung Do-wei wanted to argue back loudly.

‘Dangerous?’

Was it not dangerous for them?

The warriors and disciples of another small sect, which couldn’t compare to the Namgung family, were now dominating the battle. How could he remain silent and worry about his own safety?

“The sword troops of the Namgung sect will follow! I will protect the lord!”

“Yes!”

The young warriors of the Namgung family responded with loud cheers. Although they didn’t voice it, their eyes were also burning with fighting spirit. It was clear they shared Namgung Do-wei’s determination.

“We will never lose to Mount Hua! Be prepared to take risks and speed up!”

As the Wudang and Namgung family increased their pace, the two groups began to entangle with one another.

And then.

Taat!

“Ohhhh!”

Namgung Hwang kicked off the cliff and rose like a dragon.

‘Young bastards!’

The activities of Mount Hua were clearly visible to his eyes.

People who were less than half his age were risking their lives, and it was impossible for him, a member of a family clan, to prioritize his safety.

“Jang Ilsoo!”

Namgung Hwang, who struck down the pot of oil pouring onto his head with a single sword slash, leaped above the cliff.

“You little bastard!”

The cliff, which seemed to touch the sky, was suddenly covered in a shadow.

It was like a black sun had risen.

‘B-Black Dragon...’

“Take this!”

The Black Dragon King’s scimitar was swung in an instant, and the black-colored blade qi came like a waterfall.

Kwaaaak!

The Black Dragon King’s blade qi poured down the cliff like the spear of a god, flying straight for Namgung Hwang.

Chapter 814: I Will Handle It All, You Bastard! (4)

Namgung Hwang’s eyes were filled with shock.

An enormous amount of blade qi was pouring down over their heads. It was as if the entire field of vision was turning black.

“Tsk!”

Namgung Hwang leaped towards the blade qi, flying at him with a strike like lightning.

“I am Namgung Hwang!”

Namgung Hwang’s sword, accompanied by a mighty roar, cut through the blade qi in front of him. A sword as white as the sun sliced through the black darkness.

“Ahhhh!”

Chaakkk!

The sight of Namgung Hwang, surrounded by white sword qi, soaring through the enemy’s blade qi made the hearts of those watching him boil with excitement.

But.

“Hey!”

As the Black Dragon King’s scimitar descended once again, the blade qi, now with even more force, began to gush out.

Chak!

“Ahhhh!”

Namgung Hwang charged like a lion and split the blade qi.

The blade qi clearly demonstrated why the Black Dragon King was called the God of the Yangtze River. Still, Namgung Hwang proved his strength, earning his title as the Emperor’s Sword.

Unfortunately, this wasn’t a battle to prove an individual’s skills. That point divided the joy and sorrow between the two.

Kwaaaakk!

The Black Dragon King’s blade qi, which was split to the left and right by Namgung Hwang’s sword, rained down below without losing force.

“A-avoid it!”

“ACKKKK!”

Suddenly, the blade qi rained down on their heads, and the Shaolin and Wudang disciples flew in all directions, their faces turning pale.

But this place was on a cliff. Attacks that could have been easily avoided on flat ground were much harder to dodge here.

“ACKKK!”

“ACkkkkkk!”

Those who were swept away by the blade qi, unable to avoid it, fell helplessly, bleeding.

“Jin Heo!”

“Hae Myung! Hae Myung!”

Shaolin and Wudang.

The sects were recognized all over the world just by their names alone.

But even for them, it wasn’t easy to handle the qi of the Black Dragon King, a leader in the Shenzhou Five Sects. People climbing the cliff fell, with blood spilling all around.

What even the pouring oil and rain of arrows couldn’t accomplish, the blade qi of the Black Dragon King achieved.

“This!”

Namgung Hwang’s eyes shone.

“Black Dragon Kingggg!”

“Kuahahahaha!”

The Black Dragon King laughed loudly.

“It was worth seeing your expression, Emperor’s Sword!”

Namgung Hwang clung to the cliff again and looked down, biting his lip hard enough to bleed. Not just Shaolin and Wudang, but even the Namgung family suffered because of what he did.

When he saw the people groaning down the cliff, his stomach turned hot with anger.

But Namgung Hwang couldn’t vent his anger either.

The Evil Faction.

The Yangtze River 18 Families.

It was difficult to say that the pirates he had been dealing with so far were strong, and those weren’t empty words.

The level of their opponents wasn’t comparable to that of the Namgung family. The warriors of the pirates weren’t even strong enough to match against the elders of the Namgung family. They were inferior to the normal elders.

And so, it was true that he developed a feeling of disdain for them.

But the Black Dragon King’s blade qi was enough to shatter the pride of Namgung Hwang in one swipe.

‘Black Dragon King.’

The King of the Yangtze River, a God.

He was of a different class. At least he was completely different from the other pirates.

Namgung Hwang’s sword skills could cut through the mountain with one slash and cut through the river. But only the Black Dragon King’s blade, qi, couldn’t be cut down.

‘How?’

How could such a person emerge from among those crude pirates?

A Justice Faction nurtured all of its affiliated clans, but in a sect, people with different standards stepped in. This was the moment when one painfully realized what that meant.

“Lord Namgung!”

At that moment, Venerable Heo Do shouted loudly and passed by Namgung Hwang. Thanks to that, Namgung Hwang, who had flinched, suddenly came to his senses and gritted his teeth again.

‘Does this mean I was scared?’

The moment he saw Venerable Heo Do passing by, the blood in his body surged high.

“HEO DOOOOO!”

Namgung Hwang shouted once again and climbed up the cliff.

Beop Gye quickly followed right next to him.

Venerable Heo Do, Namgung Hwang, and Beop Gye.

Absolute warriors representing the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families climbed up the cliff in one movement to sever the heads of the leaders of their enemies.

“HAHAHAHAHAH! You little brats!”

The Black Dragon King’s eyes filled with fire.

“If you want, I will deal with all of you!”

The Black Dragon King kicked the ground and flew down the cliff. The sight of the massive human falling from the sky felt more intimidating than could be imagined.

“How dare you!”

At the moment when Venerable Heo Do gritted his teeth and was about to slash his sword.

“I will be your opponent!”

A sharp sword slash moved to the neck of Venerable Heo Do, along with a cold and creepy voice.

Kakakng!

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes shone as he gently swung the sword and pushed the sword away.

“Desolate Gold Elder!”

The Desolate Gold Elder, who had come down the cliff, stood perpendicular to the cliff as if his feet were glued to the ground and looked at Venerable Heo Do.

“If it is Venerable Heo Do of Wudang, it would be worth risking my life for. No matter how I think about it, this is a profitable business.”

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes darkened.

A man without blood or tears.

A merchant from hell who dealt with everything.

A peddler walking through the darkest places.

There were so many words to describe this man, but there was just one name that Venerable Heo Do could think of.

“First Sword of the Evil Faction.”

“All a façade. Not worth a penny.”

Venerable Heo Do adjusted the sword and held it.

‘Damn it.’

And then he glanced left and right.

In the aftermath of the fierce battle and thanks to Chung Myung’s actions, most of the flames were extinguished, but those that hadn’t been extinguished were burning at the bottom of the cliff.

There was no way to take a step back.
