The cruelty was clear, and it was nothing short of creepy. It felt like those teeth would bite the neck and kill him at any moment.

“In this entire world, is there anyone who can call me that?”

Certainly, the word ‘child’ was far from an adult man.

Especially for Hegemony Lord Jang Ilso.

“That is interesting. Still, I don’t feel that bad.”

“...”

A bright smile bloomed on Jang Ilso’s lips.

The moment they all saw that smile, the Five Sword felt an ominousness like never before.

What should they call this feeling? How should they explain this feeling that made their hearts ache with anxiety?

Jang Ilso opened his mouth in a mocking tone.

“But... wouldn’t it save your face if you just let it go after hearing that? Is it not?”

In an instant, Jang Ilso’s entire body began to rage out with qi.

Chung Myung, who had taken the qi straight on, staggered as if he would collapse at any moment.

“Cough!”

Blood flowed like a waterfall from Chung Myung’s mouth.

He had used too much of his internal qi when dealing with Jang Ilso. He briefly surpased his limits by drawing on excessive internal qi. Still, now the side effects were taking over his entire body.

“Euh...”

At that moment, Chung Myung forced out all his strength and grabbed the sword.

Tak!

Yu Yiseol stepped forward.

With a pale face and blood drained from absorbing the qi of Jang Ilso, she took one step at a time and finally stood in front of Chung Myung.

Then she raised his trembling sword and aimed it at Jang Ilso’s neck.

The eyes of Jang Ilso darkened.

It was different from when they first rushed in with enthusiasm. Now, they clearly realized that it was impossible to deal with Jang Ilso, even if all of them tried to block him.

But some people blocked him even though they knew it.

Thud!

At that time, Baek Cheon also walked out with steady feet.

He stood next to Yu Yiseol with a stern expression and pointed his sword at Jang Ilso.

“Ha...”

The same went for Yoon Jong and Jo Gul. Everyone stepped forward, surrounding Chung Myung and blocking Jang Ilso.

Jo Gul forced his lips into a smile and playfully said,

“... I think it works for now.”

“Chung Myung needs to be saved.”

“... Sasuk. Let’s take that guy and run. This place... I will handle the opponent somehow.”

“Stop speaking nonsense. I play the role of being the first to die.”

“Anyway, the insistence of Sasuk is...”

Everyone was trying to mask their trembling voices.

The jokes told with trembling legs, and bloody lips were nothing more than a pitiful tactic to desperately hide their weakness.

But...

Even Jang Ilso, who laughed and ridiculed everything in the world, couldn’t laugh this time.

It wasn’t so difficult to step forward.

A one-day-old puppy didn’t know that tigers were scary. And there were many one-day-old puppies in this world.

But that wasn’t the audacity of such a fearless person.

Courage wasn’t ignorance of fear. Not knowing fear was stupidity.

It was stepping forward even when one knew fear.

It was knowing how to take another step forward despite trembling in the face of the enemy and death.

It was knowing when to lift the sword with trembling hands.

That was what courage was.

That was when Jang Ilso felt it clearly.

The moment they blocked the front, Chung Myung’s breathing changed. Even in the face of the world’s biggest disaster, he dared to take his time, catch his breath, and begin to gather his strength again.

‘Do these guys think they can stop me for even a moment?’

There was no way that an intelligent person would think of such stupid plans. But now, Chung Myung clearly trusted those who blocked his path.

Those little brats.

“HA! HAHAHA!”

Jang Ilso smiled and looked at Chung Myung standing behind them.

“I admit it. Courage is virtuous. But... that isn’t such a good thing. Cowards survive the longest, and the courageous die first.”

“...”

“So... who is the bravest among you?”

Clench.

Jang Ilso clenched his fists. With his face, he took a step forward and intimidated everyone.

“Right, just prove your courage with your life.”

Jang Ilso’s presence began to weigh on them.

The breathing turned tight due to strong intimidation as if a typhoon was blowing. Everyone groaned under that pressure.

At that time, a twisted voice came from behind the Five Swords.

“Seriously, that nose...”

“...”

Jang Ilso’s gaze turned to Chung Myung.

Chung Myung bent his stiff neck to the side.

“For a bastard of the Evil Faction, you sure tend to talk a lot. If you think that way, it is alright to attack, but why do you keep barking like a scared dog?”

Chung Myung revealed it.

Limit? An enemy who could not be defeated?

Who cares?

‘There is no way a person who calculates things like that would have been able to climb the Hundred Thousand Mountain.’

Jang Ilso didn’t know.

No matter how many fierce battles Jang Ilso fought, no matter how many wounds he received, no matter how many times he risked his life and traversed the roads of life and death, he could never be like Chung Myung.

He didn’t know.

What was it like to fight in despair?

This was how it felt for those who had to grit their teeth and fight, even though they knew that attacking with all their might wouldn’t leave a scratch on the opponent, and they would likely lose their lives.

And... what it was like to overcome that!

“I am sorry, but.”

Chung Myung smiled, leaving behind the numerous faces that briefly passed by.

“I have been through too much to call this even a crisis.”

“...”

“So just bark and attack. I will tell you exactly why you are a kid.”

Jang Ilso stared at him in silence.

No matter how you look at it, Chung Myung was at his limit. So much so that even holding the sword must be difficult.

And yet...

‘Those eyes.’

Why do those eyes believe in his win without a doubt?

At that moment, Jang Ilso had a thought.

For the first time in his life, he met someone he couldn’t understand.

At that moment.

“Kuak...”

The cliff was steeped in silence, and a hand rose from underneath, grasping the edge of the cliff.

Everyone’s eyes immediately focused in that direction.

“Eukk....”

One by one, hands covered in dirt rose up. The Wudang and Shaolin finally succeeded in climbing the hellish cliff.

“Go up!”

“Don’t let your guard down and come up! Those on the rise, protect the rear!”

More and more people began to climb up.

Coincidentally, those who climbed the cliff naturally gathered behind Chung Myung.

Then, the Lower District troops and Black Ghost Fort, who were waiting, quickly ran behind Jang Ilso’s back and began to exude killing intent.

The entire cliff was engulfed in tension.

Chung Myung slowly turned his gaze towards Jang Ilso.

“This...”

A clear sneer on his face.

“Even though you made such a big fuss, it seems like your plan didn’t work out? What do we do? Everyone is coming up.”

“...”

“So, we should have fought while you were busy talking. If that were the case, things would have been a little different.”

There was no way Jang Ilso could refute those words. Jang Ilso’s plan was to deal with them at the bottom of the cliff, and that was shattered.

Everyone looked at Jang Ilso’s face.

This was because they couldn’t help but wonder what kind of expression Jang Ilso would make when the plan went awry.

But after a while, the expression on Jang Ilso’s face left them speechless.

The brightest smile ever.

“I admit it.”

“...”

The slow tone was oddly excited.

“This battle...”

He paused for a moment, looked at Chung Myung, and licked his lips. Then he slowly said,

“... you won.”

“...”

Hearing those unexpected words from Jang Ilso, Chung Myung had a stiff face and looked at him.

And then he spoke in a tone that seemed to suppress anger.

“... what do you mean?”

“As I said.”

Jang Ilso shrugged his shoulders. It was a face that clearly showed the calmness of the first time.

“It wasn’t in my calculations that you would be capable of occupying me so much. Clearly, you have completely distorted the plan I created. So there is no way not to acknowledge it.”

“...”

“It is my defeat. This battle is your win. Of course, the strong don’t always win the battle. This is pretty painful. Ga-Myung will be pissed.”

Chung Myung glared at Jang Ilso without the slightest agitation because what Jang Ilso wanted to say couldn’t have ended there.

“But...”

And sure enough, Jang Ilso’s lips parted slightly, showing off his white teeth.

“This is just in combat.”

“...”

“I won this war, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.”

If it had been normal, he wouldn’t have even heard those words.

But it was different now. The moment he heard Jang Ilso’s confident words, he couldn’t shake the feeling that something was amiss.

“You don’t know.”

Jang Ilso’s lips twisted into a smile.

“You should be the one who knows this best, yet you know nothing about it. That, too, is quite strange. You are indeed different.”

“What nonsense...”

“I will enlighten you.”

Jang Ilso paused, looking at Chung Myung softly. Then he spoke in a gentle tone, like a teacher addressing a favored student.

“I will tell you, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon. Just like in battle, war isn’t won by the strong... those who have something to protect will lose.”

Jang Ilso tapped his head with his finger.

“That is why you cannot defeat me. Do you understand?”

Chung Myung gripped his sword tightly.

“This bastard....”

Crack.

His body tensed, and his feet dug into the ground as if ready to charge at any moment.

It wasn’t immediately clear what Jang Ilso meant or what trick he was playing. But the moment he heard the word ‘protect,’ the faces of many people flashed in Chung Myung’s mind—from the Five Swords to Mount Hua and Hae Yeon.

And now, one thing was certain.

He had to silence Jang Ilso before he did something reckless!

As Chung Myung lowered his body like a hungry wolf, Jang Ilso smiled and shook his head.

“You don’t seem to have understood what I said at all, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon. This war...”

Jang Ilso spread his arms and declared,

“It is over.”

“Shut up, you bastard!”

In the end, Chung Myung kicked off the ground and charged at Jang Ilso. It wasn’t clear where he found the strength, but the sword qi on his blade burned brightly.

Yet Jang Ilso only smiled strangely at Chung Myung.

As if he knew the sword couldn’t reach him.

‘Why?’

It was at that exact moment that a feeling of extreme discomfort overwhelmed Chung Myung.

Paaat!

Chung Myung’s eyes widened as he rushed toward Jang Ilso. His head snapped to the side.

Kwaaang!

There was no time to react. A flying attack struck Chung Myung in the side.

“CHUNG MYUNGGGG!”

Yu Yiseol cried out, trying to rush after Chung Myung in this unimaginable situation.

“C-Chung Myung!!!!!”

Chung Myung flew out like a kite with a broken string and crashed to the ground.

The five swords ran to Chung Myung, screaming.

But just one person.

Baek Cheon turned his head and looked elsewhere with a devil-like expression.

The person responsible for Chung Myung’s plight.

The face of the one who delivered the unexpected, unpreventable blow.

“This....”

Clench.

Baek Cheon’s teeth ground together, and veins bulged in his eyes. His voice was soft, like a sob.

“Heo...Do”

And then, from his mouth, came a wild, beast-like scream.

“HEO DOOOOOO!”

Sect leader of Wudang, Venerable Heo Do.

His fury-filled eyes landed on the man.

Chapter 822: I Won This War (2)

His extended hand and clenched lips.

Everyone’s eyes were focused on Venerable Heo Do.

Not just the Five Swords but the entire Nine Great Sects in the back, and even the Evil Faction all looked at Venerable Heo Do with wide eyes.

Among them, the only one whose expression didn’t change was Jang Ilso.

“Huk... Huk, heuk.”

Heavy breathing escaped from Venerable Heo Do’s mouth. He clenched the extended hand and withdrew it, wiping the sweat off his face.

“This... you insane bastard!”

Baek Cheon gritted his teeth, exuding a killing intent as if he would lunge at Venerable Heo Do at any moment.

“What have you done! WHAT DID YOU DO! YOU...!”

Baek Cheon’s mind was so full of anger that he shouted at Wudang’s sect leader.

But Venerable Heo Do showed no reaction to the shout. He just gave a glance and walked to Jang Ilso.

He had no grudge against Chung Myung.

No, even if there was a grudge, this wasn’t the time to resolve it. There was just one reason why Venerable Heo Do attacked Chung Myung.

It was because he couldn’t stand by and watch anyone attack Jang Ilso and turn this clifftop into a battlefield again.

Venerable Heo Do stood in front of Jang Ilso and then let out a deep sigh.

Even at this time, people were climbing up the cliff, their faces a mess, and eyes shaking in despair.

Venerable Heo Do bit his lip as he watched the scene unfold. Finally, he looked straight at Jang Ilso. Bloodshot eyes. Jang Ilso simply received those hateful eyes with a smile.

A lot of attention focused on those two.

“Hegemony Lord... no, Four Precepts Alliance.”

And in the midst of it, Venerable Heo Do spoke.

“The Great Wudang Sect’s...”

His voice was suppressed as if he was holding back something.

“As the sect leader of the Great Wudang Sect... no, on behalf of the Wudang and Justice faction...”

The lips of Jang Ilso curled into a smile.

The moment that wicked smile spread across Jang Ilso’s face, Venerable Heo Do said.

“... I ask for reconciliation with the Alliance.”

A hellish silence fell upon the cliff.

Everyone looked at Heo Do as if they couldn’t understand what he said.

‘What did I just hear now?’

Reconciliation?

Did he just say that? Wudang wanted to make peace with the Four Precepts Alliance?

Even though everyone heard it, not a single person understood it. The words of reconciliation between Wudang and the Evil Faction didn’t go well together. The faces of those who faced something they had never imagined before their eyes turned pale.

It was then that Jang Ilso broke the silence.

“Hmm...”

A soft nasal sound he made, like he was enjoying this moment.

“Reconcile...”

He quietly looked at Venerable Heo Do and spoke in a voice mixed with laughter.

“I never would have thought such a thing would come out from the mouth of the noble Wudang sect leader. This is wonderful.”

“...”

Despite Jang Ilso’s taunts, Venerable Heo Do kept silent as if he would endure any humiliation.

“So... the conditions?”

“One year!”

Venerable Heo Do spat out.

“For the next year, the Five Great Families and the Nine Great Sects will not cross the Yangtze River. No, there will be no access to the Yangtze River. If only they make peace and let us cross!”

“HEO DOOOOOO!”

Only then did the desperate cry come out from Namgung Hwang’s mouth, who climbed up.

“Have you lost it? What are you talking about now?”

The anger welling up in his eyes seemed to overflow at any moment.

How could he say something like that without losing his sanity? The Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families making peace with the Evil Faction? This was something that changed the fundamentals of all the sects.

“Did you turn in fear? How dare you give in to those dirty people! And you are the sect leader of Wudang! Aren’t you embarrassed of yourself?”

Namgung Hwang’s voice was miserable, but Venerable Heo Do opened his mouth with an expression that didn’t even change.

“...stop.”

“What?”

“Shut up.”

Venerable Heo Do glared at Namgung Hwang with killing intent.

Even Namgung Hwang seemed terrified and closed his mouth. Even when facing the enemy a moment ago, Venerable Heo Do hadn’t glared with such eyes.

“... what a moron.”

Venerable Heo Do moved.

No one could understand his intention, not even Beop Gye, who climbed up late.

“Sect leader! What are you even trying to do now? Shaolin will not sit idly by and watch you do this.”

His face was devastated.

With a chilling voice, Venerable Heo Do questioned Byeop Gye.

“What?”

“...”

“So what if you don’t sit by and watch?”

“... Amitabha.”

Due to the force from Venerable Heo Do, Byeop Gye silently cried out in disapproval without even realizing it.

“Can you take responsibility?”

“... what...”

“I asked if you were confident that you would lose the disciples here and return to Shaolin and report to your abbot. No, can you handle the situation where you also might have to die with no one left to report? Can you take responsibility for that?”

“...”

Byeop Gye couldn’t respond.

Who in the world could take such responsibility? Moreover, he was an elder and not even an abbot. He couldn’t handle such a situation.

When Byeop Gye couldn’t answer, Venerable Heo Do coldly said,

“Then step back.”

“But... sect leader.”

“Do you not understand what I am saying?”

“...”

“I said step back.”

Venerable Heo Do growled.

“If you aren’t willing to take that responsibility and have no ability to resolve the situation, you should at least know when to shut up. What can be solved by barking like a dog?”

Blood flowed down his torn lip as he bit down on it.

Humiliation?

Pride?

What was the problem with that?

Now, here, his disciples were dying or alive. They had used up all their qi trying to climb the cliff; there was no way they could fend off the enemies now.

And even if they did overcome it, what would be left of them?

There would only be just a handful left. This would mean the end of Wudang.

Did it mean the extinction of Wudang in exchange for annihilating the Evil Faction?

‘Don’t make me laugh.’

If they drove out the Evil Faction and destroyed their own sect, the result would be shared among the remaining sects, Qingcheng, Shaolin, and Namgung, and Wudang would not regain their life or lose fame.

Just like Mount Hua of the past.

Mount Hua recovered its power miraculously, but there was no guarantee for Wudang to do that. No, as part of Wudang, he couldn’t even create a situation and hope for a miracle to happen.

“Reconciliation, Alliance Lord!”

Venerable Heo Do opened his mouth with red eyes.

“In return for it, I will sign a one-year non-aggression treaty. Those who stand for righteousness will not set foot in the Southlands for one year.”

“Hmmm.”

Jang Ilso tilted his head at those words.

“The Southlands... I don’t know what you mean. The Southlands is already our land.”

“You know best that it isn’t true. Right?”

“... Oh?”

Jang Ilso smiled.

But Venerable Heo couldn’t calm down yet. He bit his lip nervously and desperately said,

“The Evil Faction is a hastily formed alliance now. If it takes more damage now, you will collapse in an instant. That is probably not what you want either.”

“...”

“One year would be enough time to reorganize the Four Precepts Alliance and entirely dominate the Southlands. Isn’t that right?”

Venerable Heo Do pressed Jang Ilso, making him wear an odd look.

“If not, we will have to fight here until we both destroy one another. Is that the outcome you want, that the two sides go extinct here?”

Jang Ilso smiled as if he had won at those words, his white teeth eerily exposed.

“I think you overestimate yourself.”

“At least I don’t want to give the other people a chance to take advantage.”

“HAHAHAHA!”

Jang Ilso laughed loudly.

Advantage.

Right. If they expanded all their strength, the rest of the Nine Great Sects would start to move and head south.

Even if the Evil Faction did win against these five sects, there was no way the sects who came after could be dealt with. Naturally, a cornered mouse would bite a cat too. Those who had been deprived of their strength would suffer miserably to the end.

“You are smart, Venerable Heo Do.”

“....”

“But... It is quite weak and mean. Ah, and you are wicked too. Right, like....”

A mocking remark was given to Venerable Heo Do.

“Like the Evil Faction.”

A feeling of extreme humiliation fell onto Venerable Heo Do. His body trembled. But he didn’t think of resisting the words. The one holding the sword hilt wasn’t him, but Jang Ilso.

“Right. I think I like you. Hahahahah! The sect leader of the great Wudang is begging for his life! Hahahahah!”

Jang Ilso laughed around.

Crack.

The disciples of the Nine Great Sects standing on the cliff held back their anger and bit their lips.

It was because of extreme humiliation. No one could have imagined that they would end up begging for their lives to the Evil Faction, to live.

One had to fight and die.

That was the agreement, and that was what they learned. But no one could come forward and say that they would rather die.

It didn’t end when one person died. Fighting here meant death for all. Who would dare bear that weight?

“But... I need to know first.”

Jang Ilso stopped laughing and looked at Venerable Heo Do.

“Do you have what it takes? Do you have the strength to negotiate on behalf of the Justice faction and make them follow it?”

“If Wudang cannot, then no one can.”

“...”

“Because we live for a cause and die for a cause. And we cannot ignore the rule made in the name of Wudang and Shaolin. And if... Shaolin and Wudang, Namgung don’t step forward...”

He hesitated and then continued.

“No sect would dare step foot in the Southlands. The land ruled by the Four Precepts Alliance...”

Jang Ilso nodded as if it made sense.

“But what if that doesn’t stop?”

“... I will put my neck on the line.”

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso snorted.

“Pitiful.”

At those words, Venerable Heo Do clenched his fist tight enough to turn it white. His fingernails dug into his palms, and red blood dripped down the cracks in his fist.

“There is no way your life is worth anything, but when the sect of Wudang makes such a humble request, I feel pitiful for you and have no reason not to listen.”

“...”

“But be aware. This isn’t a non-aggression treaty but a non-negotiable, untouchable one. You are the ones who cannot cross the Yangtze River, and our Alliance isn’t restricted from crossing to the north of the Yangtze. And that period isn’t one year but will be three years.”

Venerable Heo Do’s eyes trembled.

Who could fully understand how he felt as he stood there with such pride? But Jang Ilso smiled as if everyone was looking at him.

“If you accept this...”

He spoke in a whisper and chuckled.

“I will take care of your miserable life so that you can return home alive.”

“...”

Venerable Heo Do looked back without saying a word.

The disciples of Wudang were gritting their teeth.

The courage in each of their eyes was shouting that he shouldn’t endure such humiliation, even if it meant death.

But what he had to do was already determined.

Venerable Heo Do’s hand slowly lifted.

His slightly trembling fingertips let everyone know his feelings. The trembling hands met in the air and formed a bow.

Venerable Heo Do, who slowly bowed towards Jang Ilso, lowered his head as if he couldn’t look at the sky and spoke in a whisper.

“... I accept it.”

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso nodded. Then he slowly turned to the people on the cliff. A loud and extremely harsh voice burst from his mouth.

“I, Jang Ilso, hereby declare in the name of the Four Precepts Alliance that the Nine Great Sects, the Five Great Families, and the Four Precepts Alliance have agreed to a non-aggression treaty in the Southlands for the next three years. Neither the Nine Great Sects nor the Five Great Families can set foot here! Instead...”

Jang Ilso traced his lips with his index finger and smiled.

“Go back alive. Save your miserable lives.”

“...”

Those words turned into daggers and pierced the hearts of everyone who had climbed the cliff.

“Hahahaha.”

Jang Ilso glanced at everyone and then turned around.

“Let’s go back. Seeing the losers is extremely painful to my eyes. Hahaha. HAHAHAHAHA!”

As if the rest were not worth his attention, he burst into laughter and walked away.

All of the disciples there couldn’t even raise their heads as they heard the laughter.

They wanted to blame, shout, and swear.

But they were truly aware of their shame.

No one stepped forward.

Even though something unimaginable was happening, no one got angry or raised their voice at Venerable Heo Do.

That was why they had no choice but to bow their heads.

“HAHAHAHAH! HAHAHAH!”

Tears of blood flowed from Venerable Heo Do’s eyes.

A defeat with no excuses.

A complete defeat that felt like a dagger being driven into his bone.

Chapter 823: I Won This War (3)

Only after Jang Ilso walked away did the Black Dragon King, who leisurely climbed up, move to him.

“Such a shame.”

“...”

“I could have killed him.”

Namgung Hwang’s entire face was covered in sweat and blood.

“You will definitely die in my hands, Black Dragon King.”

“What can you do when you cannot even step on the Yangtze River?”

Clench.

A sound similar to something being crushed came from the clenched teeth of Namgung Hwang. The Black Dragon King burst into laughter, seeing his opponent trembling, unable to hold back their anger.

“Showing off the pride by begging for your life and barely escaping! Right, actually, this is how the Justice Faction bastards are, hahaha!”

That was the end of his words. It would have been less shocking if he had made more explicit comments, but the Black Dragon King left with just laughter, ignoring Namgung Hwang.

“Hmm.”

The Desolate Gold Elder twitched the lips like mocking Venerable Heo Do.

“I did some extra business.”

“...”

While the Desolate Gold Elder said such words, the Thousand Face Scholar clicked his tongue.

“Tsk tsk. It is because you are so harsh.”

“... Amitabha.”

As Byeop Gye quietly chanted displeasure with a pale face, the Thousand Face Scholar bowed to Byeop Gye.

“I have learned a lot about Shaolin’s martial arts, elder.”

“...The Lower District sect’s martial arts are also... wonderful.”

“I hope we get to spar once again.”

The Thousand Face Scholar smiled bitterly. He added,

“You will have a hard time now. As you can see, our alliance lord is a tough person to handle.”

No one could respond.

Hegemony Lord, Jang Ilso.

Of course, they might have been those who ignored him until now, but starting today, the world’s view of him would change entirely.

It was clear that from this moment, no one would dare call anyone else other than Jang Ilso, the leader of the Evil Faction.

“Then.”

Finally, the Thousand Face Scholar turned.

“Let’s head back!”

“Yes!”

The Four Precepts Alliance, who left with ridicule and laughter, began to descend the cliff without hesitation.

All that was left for those who remained was devastation.

It was only then that those who climbed the cliff realized they hadn’t been able to properly even face the leaders of the Evil Faction. Unlike their miserable appearance, there wasn’t a speck of dust on their clothes.

That caused greater humiliation.

“... Heo Do.”

Namgung Hwang, who was biting his lip, glared at those walking and turned to look at Venerable Heo Do.

The anger in his eyes was so cruel that it was hard to think they were allies.

“Do you know what you just did?”

“...”

“We survived by begging for our lives to the Evil Faction. No matter how you try to make up stuff to coat it like we are friends and shit, it doesn’t change a thing!”

Venerable Heo Do looked up at the sky without a word.

“How will you resolve this? How will you pick up the honor which fell to the ground, you damned bastard!”

Just as Namgung Hwang was about to shout again, Venerable Heo Do spoke.

“Then why didn’t you stop it?”

“What?”

“I asked why you didn’t stop it.”

“...”

Namgung Hwang clenched his teeth, unable to respond.

“There were plenty of times to intervene. No, there was no need for you. Even if you led just one to attack, a war would have broken out again. Including the negotiations. Then I will ask.”

“...”

“Were you silent without knowing that?”

“This...”

The hands of Namgung Hwang were shaking. It felt like his head was burning with anger.

“Did you say honor?”

Venerable Heo Do sneered.

“Don’t worry. Because I will take responsibility. You can criticize me all you want; I will take the blame for this.”

“... Amitabha.”

Byeop Gye closed his eyes and continued to chant. He couldn’t see the face of Heo Do.

“But honor...”

Venerable Heo Do burst into a chuckle.

“It was all done with great determination, but I am sick of that hypocrisy. When I was on my knees begging for my life, you sat back and watched, and now you have no other intention but to speak of honor?”

“Sect leader...”

“No. It is right. This is the way of Kangho. But...”

Venerable Heo Do’s lifeless face looked at Namgung Hwang and Byeop Gye, then glared at Byeok Hyun.

“I will never apologize to you.”

“...”

“I won’t show gratitude either. But... at least know what you are.”

“Eik...”

Namgung Hwang’s body trembled at that.

But even so, he couldn’t bear to attack Venerable Heo Do. Because there was nothing wrong with what he said.

“... Amitabha.”

Beop Gye quietly expressed his displeasure but was saddened too.

How could this matter be resolved?

This was misery.

After finishing what he wanted to say, Venerable Heo Do took his gaze away without any regrets and turned to the sky.

‘Blue.’

The sky was mercilessly blue and cold.

He had expected those people to come help.

Apologize? Who did he have to apologize to?

He saved them all here. These were just people who didn’t dare to bow to their enemies and beg.

If he hadn’t stepped forward, everyone would have died. His actions saved them.

But they would soon criticize him and try to place all the blame on him. So why should he apologize?

‘If there’s one thing which holds me back...’

Just when Heo Do was about to sigh.

“Chu-Chung Myung!”

“Ah, no! Get up!”

Hearing the noise from the other side, Venerable Heo Do turned his head.

He could see Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon being pushed, his body being raised by his sahyungs.

A truly miserable sight. Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon had suffered the most injuries. And here, he fought more fiercely than anyone.

If he hadn’t held back Jang Ilso, the damage would have been much greater, and they would have had to offer more to Jang Ilso.

Venerable Heo Do knew that fact better than anyone.

“... Get out of the way.”

“C-Chung Myung! Now...”

“Sasuk.”

“...”

“Get out of my way, please.”

“...”

Now that he had come this far, Baek Cheon could no longer dissuade him. Chung Myung passed Baek Cheon and walked toward Venerable Heo Do. The killing intent he exuded made everyone uneasy.

The Wudang disciples held their breath as they saw Chung Myung walking away, blood dripping down his face.

“Stop.”

“You.”

Heo Sanja and Heo Gwang flew at once and stood in front of Venerable Heo Do.

But Chung Myung didn’t stop, as if he couldn’t see them. He just limped and approached Venerable Heo Do.

“Will you not stop!”

“You...”

“Move.”

Both Heo Sanja and Heo Gwang were shocked at the voice of Heo Do coming from behind.

“S-sect leader.”

“Stay back.”

“...But, but that person...”

They couldn’t back down. The killing intent of Chung Myung was unmistakable.

But Venerable Heo Do only shook his head.

“Do you want to embarrass me?”

“...”

“Get out of the way. And don’t get involved.”

Heo Gwang bit his lip.

But in the end, they couldn’t disobey his orders, so the two stepped back. They watched Chung Myung with wary eyes.

Chung Myung limped over as if he didn’t notice them and stood in front of Venerable Heo Do.

Behind him, the Five Swords were nervous, ready to spring into action if something unforeseen happened.

Venerable Heo Do smiled.

‘Mount Hua.’

On this cliff, who could speak of courage...

At that moment, Chung Myung opened his mouth.

“...Why did you do it?”

“...”

“Why?”

Venerable Heo Do burst into laughter.

“Well...”

And he mumbled.

“Why do you think?”

“You moron!”

Puak!

Chung Myung’s fist landed on Venerable Heo Do’s face. As Venerable Heo Do fell back, Chung Myung jumped on top of him and repeatedly punched his face.

“Why! Why did you! Why did you do that! Why!”

Puak! Puak!

“You do the same thing again! Again? You fucking shit!”

Chung Myung howled like an animal and hit Venerable Heo Do. The Wudang disciples tried to rush in, but when Venerable Heo Do, who fell down, raised his hand, they stopped.

“WHY!”

Chung Myung grabbed Venerable Heo Do by the collar and pulled him; their foreheads collided with a thud.

Venerable Heo Do was bleeding from the burst lip as he looked at the sky.

“I...”

A low voice came out.

“... just wanted to save my disciples.”

“...”

“That... was all.”

Chung Myung’s body shook.

“You...!”

He grabbed Venerable Heo Do by the collar with one hand and raised the other hand. His fist gathered qi on them.

“S-stop!”

“Ahhhhh!”

Kwaangggg!

A loud roar erupted. Chung Myung’s fist was struck into the ground right next to Venerable Heo Do’s face.

“Damn it...”

Chung Myung let go of Venerable Heo Do and stood up helplessly. But even after he got up, Heo Do continued to look up at the sky with blank eyes.

“You fucking morons...”

Chung Myung glared at Venerable Heo Do and the other Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families on the cliff and turned around.

As he stumbled, Baek Cheon quickly grabbed his arm and helped him.

“... let us head back, Chung Myung.”

“...”

“This isn’t our place. Let us go to the sect leader. To Mount Hua... right. Let’s go to Mount Hua.”

When Baek Cheon spoke, the Five Swords nodded. And then he looked at the other sects with a disgusted face. No one could even look at them.

While they were busy protecting their own lives, the Five Swords of Mount Hua fought against Jang Ilso.

Then how could they speak a word to them?

A warrior’s duty was to fight strong enemies. It was an agreement because they would not back down even if they could not win a fight. Fighting only when you could win was neither a will nor an agreement. It was just coercion.

‘I have listened and learned countless times.’

The difference between knowing and doing.

They fought back while the others couldn’t.

Jin Hyun looked at the sky with blank eyes and then closed them.

“Let’s go, Chung Myung.”

Baek Cheon supported Chung Myung, lifting him up. Jo Gul also supported him from the other side.

“Yes, Chung Myung... let us head back. I don’t want to stay here any longer.”

Jo Gul’s words pierced the ears of those remaining on the cliff. They were meant to be heard.

At that moment, Chung Myung spoke.

“Sasuk.”

“Uh?”

“My sword... give me my sword.”

“...why?”

Baek Cheon asked with a dissatisfied look. It was clear he didn’t want to talk anymore. But Chung Myung looked at his face and shook his head.

“Still...”

Chung Myung gazed down to the bottom of the cliff. His eyes were cold and sunken.

“There is still work to be done.”

Blood flowed down Chung Myung’s chin and dripped onto the cliff.

Chapter 824: I Won This War (4)

The Desolate Gold Elder, who had descended the cliff, glanced at Jang Ilso.

‘Hegemony Lord....’

There were two reasons why he accepted the proposal.

The first was a sense of crisis.

It was due to this sense of crisis that, if things had continued as Jang Ilso said, they might have been defeated without even taking action. Power that was built up to an excessive level was bound to be directed outward, and the best target for that power was none other than the remaining Evil Factions.

And the second reason...

‘It was calculated that there would be no loss even if he put it to good use and abandoned it.’

This made it clear.

His second thought wasn’t entirely wrong.

Although he was proud of understanding Jang Ilso to an extent, the real Jang Ilso wasn’t someone he could fully comprehend. From the time he arrived until now, nothing escaped Jang Ilso.

‘Is it actually me who got used as a piece?’

A shiver ran down his spine.

In this war, which burned like lava, the people who feared Jang Ilso the most might not have been those of the Justice faction but those who fought under him.

It felt like his heart was clenching at just the mention of it, but who else would have been this appalled?

‘Anyway...’

The Desolate Gold Elder hid his true feelings and spoke with a pretense of calmness.

“Anything left?”

“Anything left?”

The Black Dragon King’s face contorted as if he had just listened.

“That would be your position. The Yangtze River suffered losses! Above all, the fact that the Black Dragon pirates were decimated by half is a huge bl!”

The Black Dragon King glared at Jang Ilso grimly, his killing intent radiating.

“Jang Ilso! He must have said that it would bring benefits to the Yangtze River! Are you kidding me?”

“It is you... tsk tsk.”

The Thousand Faced Scholar clicked his tongue.

“I knew you were ignorant, but I didn’t realize it was to this extent. Be careful with your words, Black Dragon King. This alliance has just begun. If the dignity of the Lord is harmed, it won’t be good for us either.”

“Shut up, you rat! Didn’t you also join forces with Jang Ilso and deceive me?”

As the Black Dragon King began to show signs of aging, the Thousand Faced Scholar’s eyes turned cold.

“Tsk. Tsk.”

But then Jang Ilso smiled softly and spoke.

“There is no need to get so angry. I brought tangible benefits to the Yangtze River.”

“...Benefits? Did you just say benefits?”

“Now, no matter what you do on the Yangtze River, no one will try to stop you. For the next three years.”

The Black Dragon King frowned, looking slightly suspicious.

“But the officials...”

“It is difficult for the officials to deal with the pirates alone. Whenever they step forward, they always seek help from the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families. If they cannot proceed, the river turns a blind eye to it.”

“Hmm.”

“If that is the case, isn’t it a matter of repairing the damage? In fact, the pirates will be stronger.”

The Black Dragon King stared at Jang Ilso in silence for a moment and then cleared his throat loudly.

“That must be true, right, Alliance Lord?”

“Of course.”

“I will trust you this one time.”

As the Black Dragon King slowly retreated, the Thousand Faced Scholar asked with an accusatory tone.

“Will they keep their promise, Alliance Lord?”

“They have no choice but to uphold it. No, they will keep their promise.”

Jang Ilso smiled.

“There are no people better to play with than those obsessed with justice and respect. You don’t have to worry about it.”

“Hmm. I trust the Alliance Lord.”

The Desolate Gold Elder also spoke up.

“Then, the important thing now is to stabilize the Alliance as quickly as possible.”

“Right.”

“... then, we will make our way, organize the sects, and prepare a new strategy.”

“Right.”

“But.”

The Desolate Gold Elder slightly tilted his head and asked, his eyes dark.

“We haven’t mobilized the Blood Cult. It wouldn’t have been difficult to wipe them out there. Besides, Shaolin, Wudang, and Namgung would have been worth the damage, right?”

“Worth the damage...”

Jang Ilso smiled.

“What I want isn’t to win the battle but to win the war. Our opponent isn’t just Shaolin or Wudang, but the entire Central Plains.”

“...”

“Do you know why people are afraid of ghosts?”

“... Isn’t it because they are tough to deal with?”

“No.”

Jang Ilso shook his head.

“Because they know what a ghost is.”

“...”

“A child who doesn’t know what a ghost is will not be afraid even if he sees one. But adults have heard countless times what it is and how scary it is, and are scared of ghosts.”

The Desolate Gold Elder’s face hardened slightly.

“Soon, everyone in the world will fear me and the name of the Four Sects Alliance. From then on, things will get easier.”

The Desolate Gold Elder shook his head.

It was the same when he arrived. Even though this man said everything, he said nothing in the end.

But he was certain of one thing.

“It was a good decision to create the Alliance.”

“Hahaha.”

Jang Ilso laughed slightly.

“If you think so, don’t be obsessed with the name of Black Ghost Fort. I, too, will throw away the name of my clan.”

“... Yes. See you again. There will be a lot to discuss.”

The Desolate Gold Elder turned around as if his work was done.

Then, the Black Dragon King also turned around with a happy smile. The last remaining was Thousand Faced Scholar, who looked at Jang Ilso and laughed.

“The path to hardships is opened, Alliance Lord.”

“I guess we’ll endure it.”

“Please look after us in the future. Just know that I have no doubts in the Alliance Lord’s abilities.”

“Of course.”

“Hehehe. Sounds fun. Right.”

The Thousand Faced Scholar walked away with a mean smile on his face. As everyone dispersed, the blue qi in Jang Ilso’s eyes began to shine.

“...such cunning snakes.”

Behind Jang Ilso, who had turned away, a white carriage with eight horses was ready. Ho Ga-Myung, who was waiting quietly at the back of the carriage, greeted Jang Ilso with a cotton cloth.

“Thank you for the hard work, Clan Leader. No... Alliance Lord.”

“This is ridiculous. What kind of nonsense is this? Ugh.”

Jang Ilso groaned. Ho Ga-Myung smiled bitterly and held out a cotton cloth soaked in water.

“I guess I should have brought maids with me.”

“It’s not like I don’t have hands; it’s alright.”

Jang Ilso took the cotton cloth and wiped the blood away.

“Everything went as planned.”

“Now is the start.”

Jang Ilso answered indifferently and wiped his body, changing the cloth several times. Ho Ga-Myung took out a long white cloth from the carriage and draped it over him. Jang Ilso, who neatly organized his clothes, frowned at the broken accessories.

“Tsk.”

Clench.

He ripped off the broken accessories without regret, pushed back his flowing hair, and sighed.

“There is no way those snakes will just follow me. First, the urgent fire was put out, and from now on, they will try to devour the Four Sects Alliance.”

“It will definitely come out that way.”

“Right. That is the habit of the Evil Faction. They are the ones who pull out their swords to eat a single bean in front of them rather than a large prey afar. Those people are more problematic than the Justice Faction people.”

If Jang Ilso completely eliminated Shaolin and Wudang, what would happen next was obvious.

They would recognize Jang Ilso as their enemy, not the remaining Justice Factions. They must have united behind the scenes and fought to get rid of Jang Ilso and his clan, aiming to share the Southlands.

This was proven in the history of the Evil Faction, something that happened countless times.

“But... they cannot do that anymore, right?”

Jang Ilso gently raised his lips. A peculiar smile appeared on his face.

“The Four Precepts Alliance shouldn’t have power on its own. Only those under my control should have meaning. What I gained from this was nothing like a treaty, but the name of Jang Ilso being spread throughout the world.”

Jang Ilso gently traced his lips with his fingers.

“Fame is nothing but useless trash not worth a penny. But sometimes, it gives us more power than any violence does.”

A look of admiration passed through Ho Ga-Myung’s eyes as he looked at him.

Since when was Jang Ilso drawing this picture?

Since the time when the leaders of the Evil Faction gathered together?

Or since when Mount Hua invaded the Yangtze River?

Or was it during their ploy at the Green Forest?

No, no.

‘At least he would have foreseen this when he faced the Tang Family lord.’

So when Ho Ga-Myung tried to deal with Chung Myung, he was made to stop.

It wasn’t about looking ahead. Jang Ilso was someone who held the world in his hands and played with it.

“Now, we will have to fight hard for a while. It won’t be easy to get the valuables out of those snakes.”

“But I believe that you can do it.”

“Tsk, tsk. Ga-Myung, Ga-Myung. I heard you tend to overestimate me. I am already having a headache.”

Jang Ilso shook his head and got into the carriage. Ho Ga-Myung followed, closed the carriage door, took out the alcohol prepared in advance, and offered it.

“I have prepared it.”

“Have I ever told you that this is why I like you?”

Jang Ilso smiled.

“There is a long way to go, but we can raise a toast now. Right?”

“Of course, Alliance Lord.”

“Hahahaha!”

It was the moment when Jang Ilso was about to pour alcohol into the glass.

“Hm?”

Ho Ga-Myung turned his head to the carriage door because there was a commotion outside.

‘What is happening?’

Wasn’t the battle finished?

Jang Ilso frowned, and Ho Ga-Myung immediately spoke up.

“I will head out and see...”

But just as he attempted to rise from his seat—

Kwang!

Something sharp suddenly pierced through the carriage wall.

In that instant, cold sweat broke out over Ho Ga-Myung’s body.

The sword, so sharp it seemed capable of cutting through anything, stopped just half an inch from his face, leaving him in an awkward stance.

“...”

Had the sword advanced just an inch more, he would have been dead.

“Tsk.”

Jang Ilso clicked his tongue, having blocked the sword that had suddenly pierced through the carriage wall.

“It’s because you’re so impatient.”

He sighed, then stood up, holding the bottles.

When he opened the door and stepped outside, he saw his men surrounding the carriage, with a man standing across from them.

“Go.”

The subordinates, poised to attack the man at any moment, looked back at Jang Ilso in surprise.

“Take a step back.”

“Yes!”

The Ten Thousand People Clan retreated to the left and right, clearing the way. Jang Ilso got off the carriage and pulled out the sword that was stuck in it.

“... Is this sword a parting gift?”

“Bring it. It is quite expensive.”

“Tsk. Speak nicely.”

Jang Ilso threw the sword in his hand with a regretful look. Chung Myung, holding his Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword, sheathed it and stared at Jang Ilso.

Jang Ilso sighed lightly.

“This carriage is also expensive...”

“When you come to Shaanxi, I’ll buy you a new one.”

“In exchange, I give my head?”

“Shouldn’t you risk that much?”

“Hahahaha!”

This time, Jang Ilso threw the alcohol in his hand at Chung Myung. Chung Myung caught the bottle and tilted it to his lips.

Gulp. Gulp. Gulp.

Jang Ilso smiled, seeing Chung Myung drink it without hesitation.

“Tastes like alcohol.”

Then, he began to drink as well.

Ho Ga-Myung looked at the scene blankly.

Chung Myung suddenly rushed in and threw the sword at the carriage in which Jang Ilso was riding while Jang Ilso offered Chung Myung a drink.

He couldn’t understand their world.

The two took the bottles from their mouths and looked at each other.

A smile appeared on Jang Ilso’s face. Baring his teeth, he growled softly, like an animal threatening its prey.

“Kuak...”

That growling voice seethed with murder and hostility.

“I saved you as much as I could, so why come back? It’s not like you’re begging me to kill you.”

Then, a deep sorrow filled Chung Myung’s eyes.

Chapter 825: I Won This War (5)

“Your mouth is still alive.”

“Oh?”

Jang Ilso smiled, seemingly intrigued.

“On a subject I have no intention of delving into.”

At Chung Myung’s words, Jang Ilso’s eyes took on a strange light.

This boy was fun. Even Ho Ga-Myung, who knew Jang Ilso best, couldn’t fully read his thoughts. But this one figured out his intentions without any hesitation.

Was there anyone else in the world who was this interesting?

“There wasn’t any, right?”

“What are you saying?”

“Explosives.”

“Hahahaha.”

Jang Ilso giggled and laughed.

“It’s not like you haven’t checked it out already... A pity. Your stomach must have twisted when you saw the hole for yourself. Of course, I was disappointed not to have seen that scene.”

The lips of Chung Myung twisted.

‘You played your cards well.’

Of course, there were explosives.

It wasn’t human strength that first burst the cliff. The explosives existed for the first explosion.

But there were no other explosives.

If you thought about it, it was such a natural conclusion. It was difficult to obtain enough explosives to entirely collapse a cliff, even for the government. Obtaining it, moving it to the Yangtze River in such a short period while avoiding the eyes of people, and planting it in the right place was almost impossible based on common sense.

Still, at that moment, no one doubted whether there were explosives or not. Even Chung Myung.

There were two reasons.

First, the sight of the cliff exploding before their eyes due to the ignited explosives was impactful. There was no way those who witnessed that incredible scene could think rationally.

No, even if he had judged at that time that there was just a one-in-ten chance that explosives had been placed on the cliff, they would have had no choice but to climb the cliff.

Because no one would want to risk their lives on a one-tenth chance.

And another one...

“The fact that we gathered up the cannons and took them over before you had anything.”

“Right.”

Jang Ilso laughed excitedly.

This felt just right. Didn’t it seem like Chung Myung was slowly uncovering everything he had hidden?

No matter how great a move was made, if no one understood it, the entire effort would be useless. That’s why an outstanding person needs a rival.

“Those who came to the Yangtze River were watching your Mount Hua and every move. Of course, we knew that you had the White Thunder Cannon.”

“...”

“If you weren’t so foolish, you would have realized the moment you faced me that the cannons weren’t in my possession. So...”

Jang Ilso smiled oddly.

“Of course, you thought I had secured a lot of explosives. Thanks for that.”

“You fucking snake...”

This felt more like a compliment than an insult.

To put it bluntly, it was just that.

The Nine Great Sects, scared by the unseen explosives, desperately climbed the cliff. Thanks to that, they suffered unnecessary damage and used all their strength climbing.

“There must have been no troops on the way out?”

“Of course.”

Jang Ilso smiled and added as if amazed.

“Because we didn’t have the capacity to withdraw troops that far.”

“... What were you planning to do if they did retreat?”

“There is no other choice. We would have no choice but to turn into a dog chasing after a chicken.”

Jang Ilso shrugged his shoulders.

“But then it proves that they are dumber than we thought, so isn’t that a benefit in its own way? I could swallow those idiots at any time.”

Chung Myung distorted his face and laughed, holding his stomach.

This man was truly mad.

His stomach turned as if he had eaten something wrong. The more he looked at things, the clearer they felt.

Everyone here was fooled by Jang Ilso.

What made it even more unbearable to Chung Myung was that Mount Hua was played in the palm of Jang Ilso.

It all started by luring them to the Yangtze River. Jang Ilso must have been planning from the beginning to attract other sects by bringing Mount Hua to the Yangtze.

The Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, who never imagined that the Evil Faction would set a trap for them, all got caught in the trick.

The smile vanished suddenly, leaving only a cold expression.

“It is called the Yangtze River...”

Chung Myung gritted his teeth.

“It is a good idea to exclude the Heavenly Friends Alliance from that treaty.”

“Because there is no way you would agree to it.”

Jang Ilso giggled and laughed.

“So different from those idiots. You never back down. And you were the only one who could make that choice on behalf of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“I don’t think so?”

“Ha... Haha.”

Jang Ilso’s bright smile went stiff. Chung Myung’s words seemed to be beyond his expectations this time.

“Well, it is good. Let’s use the land handed over well. Tell this clearly to the pirates: if they even come near me, I will cut their heads off.”

“Why should I do that? I am sure you understand that the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families cannot enter the Yangtze River, right? Then why should I guarantee your land? Huh?”

“Because that is what you want?”

“...”

Jang Ilso frowned.

Truth.

A moment of deep sorrow leaked out between his narrowed eyes.

“Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon. Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon...”

Jang Ilso, who had been mumbling, shook his head.

“That name is lacking for you now. It makes my heart go cold.”

“There is no need to reignite the heart. All your tricks are obvious.”

What Jang Ilso wanted was the complete downfall of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, with the Heavenly Friends Alliance not even in his sight.

In terms of power, even if he combined the Heavenly Friends Alliance and the Four Precepts Alliance, they would be similar or rather inferior to the other Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families.

Since there was no possibility of these two joining hands, even this one victory wouldn’t put the Evil Faction at ease.

And so, this non-aggression pact bought time. And...

‘Entirely destroy the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families.’

The rumor that the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families were defeated and had to retreat from the Yangtze River would soon spread throughout the world. And the rumor of them begging for their lives would also emerge.

Because Jang Ilso will spread it directly.

Criticism would reach the sky, and the reputation they had built and the honor they had polished would be thrown into the dirt.

But in the meantime... What if the Heavenly Friends Alliance stood proudly on the Yangtze River?

What if they protected the civilians?

‘It would be hell for the Nine Great Sects.’

If even the Heavenly Friends Alliance gave in, there would be public opinion on it. But if the rumor spread that the Heavenly Friends Alliance fought till the end, no one would be able to put the two letters, non-aggression, behind the name of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families.

In other words, the person who most desperately needed to praise the Heavenly Friends Alliance right now was none other than Jang Ilso.

“Tsk. You are no fun. It should be a gift given to someone scared and trying to run.”

Jang Ilso took a sip of the drink and clicked his tongue.

“The pirates will not approach the land you occupied, let alone your mouth. If those Nine Great Sects block it, saying they will kill you and me, or if that Black Dragon King goes too far to block the commercial districts of the Southlands, I will be in a mess.”

“You speak so stupidly. Even after hearing that, do you think I will let ships pass for pennies?”

“You are planning to suck them dry, right? Is it not?”

Chung Myung smiled, showing his teeth.

Of course, his stomach churned to the point where his teeth were grinding.

But looking at it calmly, the Heavenly Friends Alliance and Mount Hua lost nothing in this war. They gained a lot.

The Heavenly Friends Alliance would usurp the position of the Nine Great Sects, who previously represented Kangho, and would make a huge profit by monopolizing the water path to and from the Southlands.

In return, the Evil Faction weakened the strength of the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families, buying time to reorganize.

A hostile-symbiotic relationship.

There couldn’t be a more appropriate expression for their ties.

“What do you think, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon?”

“... What?”

“Do you like my gift?”

“...”

“You cannot refuse my gift. No, there is no way to refuse. But you don’t have to be grateful. I have received too many gifts from you. Hahahaha!”

Chung Myung’s smile deepened further.

“I will tell you something, too.”

“Hmm?”

Jang Ilso looked at Chung Myung with a smile on his face as if he was impatiently curious about what was coming.

“You said it right. You won this war.”

“Hmm?”

“But it is only a war.”

“...”

Jang Ilso’s eyes darkened.

“You will find out what a stupid thing you did.”

“... Stupid?”

“Battles are small things compared to war.”

“Yes.”

“But war is nothing compared to fortune.”

“..”

“If you really wanted to take control of Kangho, you should have come out of here and destroyed Mount Hua at any cost. You are the one who missed the big thing because you were distracted by the small fry in front of you.”

“...”

“You will find out.”

A terrifying killing intent flowed from Chung Myung’s body.

“What does it mean to give time to Mount Hua? It won’t be that long. The moment your head is cut, you will definitely regret this day and regret it all. Surely.”

“...”

Jang Ilso’s face turned cold.

Simple threats... No, it might just be a struggle.

But...

To Jang Ilso’s ears, those words didn’t sound so simple.

They were kept alive because Chung Myung was essential for his plan. His existence balanced the Heavenly Friends Alliance, the Four Precepts Alliance, the Nine Great Sects, and the Five Great Families.

If Chung Myung ceased to exist, then the Heavenly Friends Alliance would collapse, and the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, with no one to keep them in check, would come to the south.

But... on the other hand, his instincts were speaking.

Maybe leaving this guy alive might be more dangerous than facing the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families.

It was exactly as this boy said.

Clench.

Jang Ilso clenched his fists without realizing it.

‘Even now...’

It was the exact moment when Jang Ilso bit his lip in a boiling mood.

Suddenly, he saw a group approaching.

‘That?’

Jang Ilso’s eyes widened. It was still too far away to be visible clearly, but what was approaching was coming at an incredible speed.

‘Green?’

The moment he saw the green robes they had on, Jang Ilso could tell the identity of those who were approaching.

“Tsk...”

Jang Ilso, having lost his mood, unclenched his fist. No matter how skilled he was, it was impossible to kill Chung Myung before they arrived. And if a battle broke out, he would be at a disadvantage.

If that happened...

“... so it was me who saved your life?”

“If that idiot Heo Do hadn’t done something stupid.”

The Sichuan Tang family. They were swiftly approaching.

If they joined the battle, the entire result would have been different.

“... when did you call them?”

“I had no surety of what would happen in this world.”

Jang Ilso bit his tongue and licked his lips.

‘It was dangerous.’

He almost got cornered again. This guy was truly dangerous.

“I like it.”

Jang Ilso finished the drink in his hand and looked at Chung Myung.

“No matter what, I won the battle this time.”

“Right. So enjoy it.”

Chung Myung smiled, showing his teeth.

“That moment of joy won’t last long.”

The eyes of the two people staring at each other were filled with killing intent.

“Huh.”

Jang Ilso, who snorted lightly, turned first and got into the carriage.

“Let’s leave.”

“Yes.”

In the carriage, which began to move, Jang Ilso leaned his head against the wall with a strange look.

‘I feel frustrated.’

He thought it was a complete victory. But... now that he saw it, wasn’t it just a lucky win? If the Justice Faction had just held out until the Sichuan Tang family arrived, the Four Precepts Alliance wouldn’t have been able to take advantage of the war and negotiate.

“Alcohol tastes bad.”

A low mumble escaped from red lips. Jang Ilso’s eyes narrowed like a snake.

‘Mount Hua... and Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon, Chung Myung.’

It was the moment when Jang Ilso engraved that name into his heart.

“I need to prepare a suitable name. It is difficult for Jang Ilso’s enemy to be called something like a brat.”

After a while, the soft laugh he let out slowly spread beyond the carriage and into the quiet ground.

Chapter 826: Where Are The Others Now? (1)

The white carriage and the Ten Thousand People clan following it drove away.

Tang Gunak, who arrived with the Tang family, sighed softly at this sight.

“... I guess we ended up coming too late.”

“No.”

Chung Myung shook his head.

“You came right on time.”

“Your body...”

“Well, it is fine.”

Although the word ‘rag’ couldn’t be more appropriate, Chung Myung smiled calmly.

Actually, to him, these injuries weren’t a huge deal compared to the past. It wasn’t like his limbs fell off.

“... I brought several people who are excellent at healing, so you should get treatment first.”

“No. It is fine. Rather...”

As Chung Myung tried to turn around, Tang Gunak grabbed him by the shoulder.

“...”

When Chung Myung looked around unsure, he repeated with a stern look.

“Get treatment first.”

“...”

“What are you doing?”

“Yes, lord!”

As the elder of a Tang family with a white cloth wrapped around his shoulders came running, Chung Myung licked his lips with a bit of an embarrassed look.

“I will get treatment. I will really get treatment. But not now. Let us organize the other things first.”

“Is it urgent?”

“Rather than urgent... because it is something I have to do.”

Seeing the look in Chung Myung’s eyes, Tang Gunak hesitated in displeasure but eventually reluctantly nodded.

“I understand. Just remember one thing. Your body isn’t just yours. That is what leading people is all about.”

“...”

“Why do you look at me like that?”

“No, well...”

Chung Myung scratched the back of his head.

“It is nothing.”

This was something Chung Myung heard quite often in the past.

-Yah! Do you think your body is yours alone!

‘Nagging...’

He never thought he would hear those obvious naggings again here. Perhaps because he had just experienced something urgent, the tip of his nose felt numb.

“Fine. How was it, Jang Ilso and all?”

“... he is a snake.”

“A big snake at that. The world calls giant snakes dragons.”

“That dragon can freeze to death. Right, he is a snake.”

Chung Myung glared at Jang Ilso’s retreating carriage, now just a faint dot in the distance.

‘Do as much as you want now.’

But the next time they met, that smile would vanish.

“Tsk.”

Chung Myung briefly clicked his tongue and turned around in frustration.

“Let us climb up. The sect leader is waiting there.”

“I understand. But what happened? Looking at your condition, it seems like a big fight. Why did Jang Ilso just retreat? He didn’t seem to be harmed enough to retreat.”

“That...”

Chung Myung searched for words, scratching the back of his head, and sighed.

“It is a bit complicated to explain.”

Tang Gunak’s eyes were filled with doubt.

“Chung Myung!”

The Five Swords, who had been waiting anxiously, were startled by the sight of Chung Myung climbing the cliff again and running towards him.

They had said they would come, but they had no choice but to wait there because Chung Myung insisted that if they all went, it would lead to another fight. And he wasn’t the type to be stopped, even if they went along.

“Is it alright? Did that bastard Jang Ilso try something again?”

“What the hell! If you don’t want to be fooled, at least keep your mouth shut!”

“... I really don’t get what you are.”

After examining Chung Myung’s physical condition, they all took a deep breath, relieved to find no new injuries.

“What are you even doing?”

“Nothing. I was upset to just let him go, so I decided to at least shout out.”

“... insane bastard, please...”

Baek Cheon held his throbbing head. At that moment, Hyun Jong approached from behind them.

Chung Myung spoke awkwardly as he looked at Hyun Jong, who was right in front of him.

“Sect leader...”

Hyun Jong bit his lip tightly.

Looking at Chung Myung’s face, he felt as if his insides were burning and he was being sliced into pieces.

He placed his trembling hands on both sides of Chung Myung’s shoulders.

“... you did great.”

Still, he bowed his head slightly. It was difficult to see Chung Myung’s face.

“Really... you really worked hard... I have no shame...”

He could do nothing because the Ten Thousand People Clan blocked them. He believed the best choice was to reduce the damage to the other disciples, but seeing Chung Myung in front of him, even those words felt useless.

Was it right to force this child to make sacrifices to protect others?

“I am...”

At that moment, Chung Myung held Hyun Jong’s hand, which was on his shoulder.

“You were great.”

“...”

“If the sect leader had been excited, everyone wouldn’t have been safe. Thanks to the sect leader being there, I could fight in peace.”

Hyun Jong couldn’t bear to say anything more and closed his eyes tightly.

‘Damned boy.’

Why did he think of others even in such a situation? The person who suffered the most here was none other than Chung Myung himself.

‘This kid has been through this hell every time.’

It made him think again.

The fact that Mount Hua might have been created by the blood shed by Chung Myung. Hyun Jong’s insides burned as he faced a reality that he couldn’t ignore.

“I...”

“Sect leader. Lord Tang has come.”

A deep sigh escaped Hyun Jong’s mouth as he stopped trying to say something.

When he raised his head, he saw Tang Gunak standing a little behind. As Chung Myung moved to the side to clear the way, Hyun Jong calmly greeted the Tang family head.

“It has been a while since I saw you, Lord Tang.”

“I greet you, sect leader.”

Tang Gunak politely bowed.

“Sorry. I arrived too late...”

“No. Thank you for coming here. But how did you know...”

“I received a message from Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.”

When Hyun Jong turned as if to check, Chung Myung scratched the back of his head with a flustered look on his face.

“Something was happening in a delicate situation. If you make a fuss, you will lose strength, but it is better than being trapped in a dangerous time. I am sorry for not telling you in advance.”

“No, no.”

Hyun Jong shook his head.

It never occurred to him that the situation was strange until he came here. So it would have been difficult to inform.

‘We are holding his ankles.’

Both he and the current Mount Hua were still insufficient for Chung Myung.

“I heard about the situation on the way here, Alliance leader.”

Hyun Jong sighed softly at those words. This was because he felt that Tang Gunak’s voice was colder and stiffer than usual.

“...where were you?”

Tang Gunak bit his lip and asked again.

“Where are the others now?”

“Ugh...”

“Sajae, you alright?”

Those who came back down were taking care of the injured. Every time they saw the corpses around, an indescribable look of devastation appeared on their faces.

“S-sahyung...”

One of the Wudang disciples, who was injured and writhing, grabbed the arm of the helper and squeezed out a voice.

“Evil... Evil Faction bastards...”

“...”

They couldn’t bear to answer. The person who closed their eyes tightly turned their head and answered briefly.

“First, handle your body state first.”

“...S-sahyung...”

“Move to the ship.”

“Yes.”

Those carrying the wounded quickly moved to the anchored ship.

Jin Hyun, who was watching this, closed his eyes without realizing it.

The agreement was based on sacrifice. Those who claim the name of righteousness and justice must know how to abandon their lives for the sake of justice. That is what they heard.

Then... what had all these people here died for?

They failed to do their job, and they failed to stay right.

So what else did the remaining people protect? In the end, all those alive at the end had pitifully saved their lives.

“Hurry!”

Namgung Hwang’s voice came out cold.

“We must finish the settlement before the sun sets!”

“Yes.”

The members of the Namgung family responded, but unlike when they first arrived, their voices lacked strength.

Of course, Namgung Hwang couldn’t blame them either.

Clench.

He clenched his fist and looked around. He saw the survivors of the Black Dragon pirates glaring at him as if watching.

His teeth ground together.

Those hateful bastards were laughing at them, but Namgung Hwang couldn’t do anything about it. They had already lost. In a situation like that, taking out his anger on them would only make him look more foolish.

‘Yangtze River.’

There might be no need for a treaty.

Because he would not step on the Yangtze River on his own again. If he saw the Yangtze River flowing with his eyes, it was obvious that the humiliation of the day would come back to him.

“Lord, the settlement is done.”

“And the bodies?”

“... respectfully done...”

Namgung Hwang nodded slowly.

If he thought about it, the damage wasn’t so severe. Although those who fell down the cliff sustained serious injuries, the actual number of those who died wasn’t large.

Namgung Hwang’s eyes were fixed on one place.

The back of a person who was busy pushing away fallen rocks and tending to the injured.

It wasn’t often for him to express gratitude to others, but at this moment, he truly felt grateful to the man. If he hadn’t kept the Black Dragon pirates in check from below, all those who had fallen and been injured would have been dead at the hands of those Black Dragon pirates.

“... was it Hae Yeon?”

“Yes. The one who walked with Mount Hua here...”

“Is it Mount Hua again?”

Why did it end up like this?

Clearly, they were all full of spirit when they entered. But now he felt like a dog with its tail between its legs, heading home helplessly.

Namgung Hwang suddenly looked at the culprit of the incident.

Venerable Heo Do.

He could clearly see him taking care of the Wudang disciples and putting them on the ship.

Clench.

Namgung Hwang gritted his teeth as he spoke.

“Now, the entire world will point its fingers at us.”

“Lord...”

“How on this world do I bear such humiliation?”

“There will come a time for redemption. It is said that it isn’t too late for the right man to take revenge, even after ten years. One day, you will cut off the head of that wicked Four Precepts Alliance leader and repay the debt.”

“... damn it.”

It was then.

“Hmm?”

Namgung Hwang turned his head at the unfamiliar presence he felt for a moment. Several people were coming down from the top of the cliff.

‘Is it Mount Hua?’

His face contorted at that.

He didn’t want to see Mount Hua right now.

Since he was human, it was natural for him to be grateful, but this feeling was different from that. As he was a rather shameful person, it was tough for him to look up at Mount Hua.

But... Namgung Hwang’s face contorted even more as he looked ahead.

The first thing he saw was the familiar face of Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon, Chung Myung. And the one following...

Namgung Hwang’s face turned pale as he recognized the man.

Perhaps there was someone there at this moment that he almost didn’t want to see in the world.

Tak.

He descended to the bottom of the cliff and slowly walked ahead.

A cold, cold face as if covered in armor.

Deer skin gloves exposed beneath and the wide green sleeves of the robes.

“Tang... Gunak...”

A voice like a groan came out from Namgung Hwang’s mouth.

Tang Gunak, the Sichuan Tang family lord, walked straight to him.

Step. Step. Step.

Tang Gunak, who was standing in front, slowly looked around and then looked at Namgung Hwang.

And his mouth slowly opened.

“... is...”

“...”

“Is this the choice of the Five Great Families?”

Tang Gunak’s face contorted like a demon.

“This... you morons who don’t even know what pirates are!”

Namgung Hwang closed his eyes tightly, overwhelmed by the unfiltered anger pouring out from Tang Gunak.

Chapter 827: Where Are The Others Now? (2)

Tang Gunak’s icy eyes seemed to pierce through Namgung Hwang.

‘Why here...’

Why did Tang Gunak, who was supposed to be in distant Sichuan, appear on the Yangtze River? And why now, of all times?

Namgung Hwang’s fingertips began to tremble slightly.

Of course, everyone here probably didn’t want to face anyone else. They knew very well what they had done.

However, facing Tang Gunak and not someone else in this situation was, for Namgung Hwang, a terrible experience incomparable to anything else.

The Namgung family of Anhui and the Sichuan Tang family of Sichuan.

The two sects were the two keystones that led the Five Great Families for a long time. Naturally, the heads of both families had competed, cooperated, and even fought with each other for a long time.

From Namgung Hwang’s point of view, the person he didn’t want to lose to even more than the Nine Great Sects was Tang Gunak.

“Answer me, Namgung Hwang.”

Tang Gunak scolded in a cold tone.

“What on earth is the agreement you spoke so loudly about?”

“...”

“And yet you are the head of the Blue Heaven Namgung family? After you beg for your life from the Evil Faction, can you still talk about your people being the Blue Heaven with your own mouth?”

Namgung Hwang bit down on his lip until he bled.

What caused him the most pain wasn’t the rebuke of Tang Gunak. Even after hearing that reprimand, he couldn’t make a single excuse.

“Answer me.”

“...”

‘Was that life so important? To the extent of throwing the value of justice to the ground?’

“... Tang Gunak.”

Tang Gunak had changed. He wasn’t mocking Namgung Hwang. He was genuinely angry.

Although the Tang family had become a member of the Heavenly Friends Alliance and their ties had become complicated, the Sichuan Tang family was still one of the Five Great Families. The indecency shown by the Namgung family, which was the head of the five families, made his blood boil.

No, it wasn’t just the Five Great Families.

“Answer me, monk.”

“... Amitabha.”

Byeop Gye, who received the gaze of Tang Gunak, couldn’t bear to face him and just chanted with his eyes closed.

Even after witnessing this helpless reaction, the intensity of Tang Gunak’s force didn’t diminish one bit.

“Ridiculous.”

A cold cynicism flowed through everyone’s hearts.

“I never would have thought that those who repeatedly emphasized that justice is more important than life would throw away their justice and act as if they weren’t devoted partners the moment they got the chance. Are you all still qualified to call yourselves just and righteous? And be still?”

Byeop Gye’s face turned red.

Even if he had ten mouths, what could he have said?

Byeok Hyun, the sect leader of the Qingcheng sect, which was located in Sichuan and had some contact with Tang Gunak, spoke with a bitter tone.

“... Please stop, Lord Tang. All of this was done by Venerable Heo Do. Namgung Hwang and Monk Byeop Gye are no different from victims of this incident.”

“Victims?”

However, there was a sneer on the lips of Tang Gunak.

“They are innocent because they didn’t come forward and shout?”

“That...”

“Let me ask you something.”

Tang Gunak glared at everyone with eyes filled with killing intent.

“Then why are you alive?”

“...”

Those words left Byeok Hyun silent.

“Save your disciples? Fine. That is totally understandable. But then, why are you alive with your feet on the ground? And without injuries at all!”

The strong voice of Tang Gunak burst out loud.

“What on earth did you people do while the young people of Mount Hua were bleeding and fighting? Who is it that should have been shedding their blood and fighting here?”

Namgung Hwang closed his eyes and took a deep breath through his nose.

Saying that he was unable to focus on winning because he was watching the situation was nothing more than a poor excuse.

He had lived this long and didn’t know it.

What it meant to fight for your life and how difficult it was.

Of course, he might have to force himself to make an excuse.

However, the scars that covered Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon had made his tongue stiff and weighed heavily on his lips.

“The reason the world tolerates your violence is because it believes there is justice at the tip of your sword. The moment you negotiate for your own benefit....”

Tang Gunak chewed over the words as if spitting them out.

“It is no different from being a thief. You moronic bastards.”

“...”

There was silence. Tang Gunak bit his lip, holding back a sigh.

No matter how much he cursed, he could not undo what had happened. Knowing that made him even more frustrated.

After barely calming down, he gritted his teeth and said,

“I declare this as Lord of the Sichuan Tang family. From today onwards, the Sichuan Tang family will never be called one of the Five Great Families.”

“Tang Gunak!”

“Shut up, you damn bastard.”

When Namgung Hwang shouted in shock, Tang Gunak snarled back like a tiger.

“What you started out of envy and jealousy has ended in fear and cowardice.”

Tang Gunak glared at everyone around with cold eyes and then turned.

“Get out of here. Now, the Yangtze River is a place you cannot step on.”

Namgung Hwang’s body trembled.

A terrible sense of humiliation and unbearable shame took over his entire being.

But what was even bigger than that was the disgust with himself.

“...Get on board.”

“... Yes.”

In the end, when Namgung Hwang spoke weakly, the members of the Namgung family answered softly and headed to the ship anchored nearby.

“Amitabha.”

Byeop Gye also shook his head and sighed deeply.

“... Let’s go back.”

The Shaolin monks nodded slightly towards the ship. However, among those turning around, just one person was standing with his feet on the ground.

“...What are you doing?”

“Elder.”

Hae Yeon opened his mouth with a calm face.

“I will not come back.”

“...”

Anger surged onto the face of Byeop Gye.

He was now the acting abbot of the clan. Disobeying his orders was disobedience to the abbot and to all of Shaolin. There was no way Hae Yeon didn’t realize it.

But Hae Yeon gazed at Byeop Gye with unwavering eyes. His anger was short-lived, and Byeop Gye instantly lost strength at the sight of his calm face.

Everyone quickly climbed up the cliff to survive.

At that time, Hae Yeon was the only one who stayed below to watch over those climbing up.

In this terrible hell, Hae Yeon was the only one who put into practice the Shaolin teachings of compassion and altruism. By what right did Byeop Gye have to criticize him?

“... Do whatever you want.”

“Amitabha.”

Hae Yeon calmly chanted. Byeop Gye headed to the ship without looking back any further.

Byeok Hyun, who was left alone, opened his mouth towards Tang Gunak.

“Lord Tang...”

“Now.”

But Tang Gunak didn’t even glance at him and scolded him coldly.

“I do not want to talk now, so please head back, sect leader.”

“...”

A deep exhale escaped from Byeok Hyun’s mouth.

‘It must hurt.’

Tang Gunak was a person who understood the heavy pressure of having to take responsibility for the lives of countless disciples. Even such a person as Tang Gunak reacted with such contempt. How cold and abandoned will the reaction of the people around the world be?

Just thinking about it made his fingers go cold.

“... till the next time. Do take care.”

Byeok Hyun, who left only those words in a miserable mood, led the disciples to the ship.

Those walking to the ship, their legs held no strength. It looked quite different from when they came. It was natural. They had lost so much here.

Shaolin’s ship was the first to enter the narrow valley, followed by Namgung’s ship leaving the canyon. Eventually, even the ship of Qingcheng turned its bow, but the ship of Wudang, which was the first to be anchored, didn’t move and stayed anchored.

A person who was not on board was standing in front of Wudang’s ship, looking their way.

“That man...”

When Tang Gunak tried to move ahead, Chung Myung grabbed his arm and stopped him.

“Wait.”

Then he approached the person standing in front of Wudang’s ship. Venerable Heo Do, who stood alone on the land, glanced at Chung Myung approaching and smiled.

“Don’t be so upset, Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.”

“...”

“Nothing bad has happened to the Heavenly Friends Alliance. You wouldn’t want to miss a time when the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families are criticized. How about being honest and happy instead?”

Chung Myung stared at Heo Do without answering.

“... why? Do you still have anything left to say?”

When Heo Do asked, Chung Myung looked at him with dark eyes and then spoke after a pause.

“Idiot.”

It was a cold day.

“If you have made a choice, grit your teeth and stick with it. There is nothing more foolish than doubting your own choices. And if you use an evil move, no one will criticize you more than needed, and hurling insults at the face won’t make you feel any ease.”

“...”

“Right, you did the dumbest thing in the world. You will live the rest of your life regretting the choices you made. And not only you but the entire Wudang and Nine Great Sects will be held responsible for that.”
