At that time, Chung Myung poked him on the side.

“Ah, we are in Xi’an now.”

“R-right! We are! There are many!”

“...”

Hong Dae-Kwang felt like he had asked for something he couldn’t say, so he continued to speak hesitantly, avoiding the gaze of Hyun Jong.

“Yes... that, uh... in any case, just because there are sects lined up, the higher sect cannot give instructions to the lower clans. Isn’t that something which cannot be done even to the sub-sects of a sect because of the principles of the Justice faction?”

“Yes.”

“The reason for lining up is simply to gain an advantage. The same goes for the Evil Faction. But what the Four Precepts Alliance is saying now is not just to bring the lower clans into line. The idea is to subjugate them and make them their slaves. The idea is to bring together the many sects in the world that are tagged as Evil under the name of the Evil Faction. In other words....”

Hong Dae-Kwang bit his lip and said.

“Only the Evil Faction will remain in the world; the Evil Faction will be the Four Precepts Alliance, and the Four Precepts Alliance will become the Evil Faction.”

“W-what is that?”

Hyun Jong’s face turned slightly pale. Only now did they understand how tremendous the declaration of unification was.

“Is that possible?”

“Of course, it isn’t. If it were possible, there would have been countless people who would have attempted such a thing by now. But...”

When Hong Dae-Kwang hesitated to speak, Chung Myung took over.

“That very impossible thing is possible now.”

“Hm?”

“With the emergence of the Four Precepts Alliance, the powerful sects are no longer able to keep each other in check, and the Justice faction, who step in at the right time and take advantage of the situation, are also unable to approach this land.”

“Hmm... that is...”

“But.”

Chung Myung smiled and laughed.

‘I thought they would be busy handling the internal aspects... so they are going to come out like this? The land where the knife was cut probably didn’t even heal yet, and yet Jang Ilso, that bastard....“

In Chung Myung’s opinion, the current Jang Ilso was no different from a greedy pig. No matter how great he was, there was no way he could swallow the entire pirates, Black Ghost Fort, and Lower District Sect at the same time.

So he thought he would have a hard time sorting things out, but it turned out to be the opposite of what Chung Myung expected.

“What do you think will happen?”

“Hmm.”

Im So-Byeong, receiving Chung Myung’s gaze, scratched his chin.

“Actually... although this is extreme, it is a reasonable number. When there is conflict internally, it is best to create enemies on the outside.”

“Right.”

“If the internal reorganization continues like this, there will eventually be a power struggle among the Four Sects Alliance. But if they turn their knives outside, whether they are strong or weak, they won’t be biting down on each other’s throats for a while. And that could be what Jang Ilso is aiming for.”

Chung Myung’s eyes sunk in.

“I don’t think that is all.”

“Uh?”

“Unification of the Evil Faction. This war is none other than a war carried out in the name of the Evil Faction.”

“... isn’t that obvious?”

“In other words, those who subjugate are also subjugated in the name of the Evil Faction.”

“Ah!”

Im So-Byeong flinched as if he had missed that.

“Right, then....”

Hyun Jong, unable to follow their conversation, asked with a stern look.

“Chung Myung. What does that mean?”

“When the Shenzhou Five Sects subjugate the Evil Faction sects in each land, the subjugated sects’ lands come under them. But if they subjugate in the name of the Four Precepts Alliance, they will follow the orders. And so...”

“... are you saying that all of that power will turn up under Jang Ilso, the Alliance leader? Everyone coming under his command from now on?”

“Precisely.”

Chung Myung smiled, showing his teeth.

“A crazy person is doing a crazy thing.”

In every aspect of the world, there were stages. The reason you needed to take that step was that the consequences of ignoring the rule were significant.

They had just achieved a form of union, yet they were enriching themselves while neglecting internal maintenance? And leading the other sects of the Shenzhou Five Sects, which could not be considered as much, to completely surrender to him?

This was no different from a blatant provocation directed at the other leaders of the Shenzhou sects.

Baek Cheon asked with a stern face.

“Of course, there will be backlash, right?”

“You must have the confidence to press that, right? If it succeeds, the benefits will surely be great.”

No matter how great the sects belonging to the Shenzhou Five Sects were, if it attracted all the other sects, its power could not be taken lightly. No, at least in terms of numbers, it would be more than enough to overwhelm the other sects.

If that happened, even the Black Dragon King, Thousand Faced Scholar, and Black Ghost Fort leaders wouldn’t dare touch Jang Ilso.

“And if they fail?”

“Death.”

Chung Myung giggled.

“He will die the most gruesome death without leaving a single piece of flesh behind. He probably knows that.”

All small and medium-sized sects that protest would be trampled and subjugated. In the meantime, he must keep in check the other leaders of the Shenzhou Five Sects, who might try to stab him in the back at any time.

This was an incredibly dangerous tightrope Jang Ilso chose to walk on.

But now that the lingering effects of war hadn’t subsided yet, Jang Ilso had declared that he would continue on this tightrope.

“What do you think? The odds of success?”

Chung Myung frowned at Baek Cheon’s question.

“... can he? No, maybe... half and half?”

“Half?”

At that level, it was a close gamble and even he would not be sure of winning.

“That Jang Ilso?”

Baek Cheon unconsciously made a remark about Jang Ilso, but there was no need to change his words.

“I am giving him that much chance because it is Jang Ilso. If it were any other guy, he would have already died and turned into dog food by now.”

On the contrary, Chung Myung held Jang Ilso in high regard.

“Then...”

Baek Cheon gulped and opened his mouth. He was torn between asking immediately or waiting, but he couldn’t hold back.

“... What if Jang Ilso succeeds despite the slim odds? Then what happens?”

“What if he succeeds?”

“Yes, then what?”

Chung Myung twisted his lips into a smile.

“On the outside, it might look like the Four Precept Alliance has unified the Evil Way, but in reality, it will be like the Ten Thousand People Clan swallowing the Evil Faction. In other words...“

“...”

“An unprecedented Evil Sect, which has never appeared before, will be born.”

Chills ran down Baek Cheon’s spine.

“And Jang Ilso will become the only person to lead the greatest sects of all. This has never happened before in the history of Kangho.”

A dead silence fell in the hall.

It was such a huge statement that they had no idea how to react.

Chung Myung’s low laughter echoed through the silence.

People looked at Chung Myung with blank faces.

“An insane person just does insane things. If you’re not going to get everything, it doesn’t matter if you don’t get torn, right?”

For the moment, Jang Ilso would have endured again and again.

Now, Jang Ilso had started to move.

He would never stop until he devoured the entire world or burned it down. Even if he swallowed too much, his stomach exploded, and he died, or even if he burned himself from the fire he made!

Because that was the way people like Jang Ilso lived their lives.

Meanwhile, Baek Cheon’s face, looking at Chung Myung, was stiff.

Everyone was weighed down by the gravity of Jang Ilso’s actions, but only Chung Myung seemed happy.

Maybe it was an illusion...

“I am curious, too.”

At that moment, Chung Myung spoke with a bizarre smile.

“Is Jang Ilso really capable of keeping the entire Evil Faction under his command, or is he just an idiot who will explode and puke out everything he inhales?”

Chung Myung’s gaze turned to the door. To be exact, beyond the door.

Until now, the world seemed to be waiting for Chung Myung to make a move. But now, the world is starting to come upon Chung Myung.

‘This is how it stays interesting.’

Chung Myung clenched his fists.

“But... well, the result is the same. I will prove to him that the mistakes he makes are irreversible.”

He fixed his gaze to the south and smiled, showing his teeth.

And maybe.

Someone far away, out of sight, might be looking at them and smiling.

Chapter 840: The Agreement Papers Are Still There (5)

Step. Step. Step.

Splash.

Pure white silk shoes stepped into a puddle of blood.

The man frowned in displeasure as his footwear was stained by the blood that splashed up.

“...this is why I hate blood.”

The man, who shakily stepped out, raised his head and looked ahead. His face, once irritated, soon filled with joy.

“But... I like red.”

The whole world was turning red.

The ground was soaked with blood that flowed endlessly, and bloodied corpses were lined up. Moreover, as the flames burning the palace soared, even the black sky was turning red.

The only thing there that wasn’t red was the man.

“Ugh... uhh...”

Jang Ilso, a man whose entire body was wrapped in a long white cloth, turned his head towards the source of the groans.

A man with one arm and leg cut off was writhing and groaning in a puddle of blood.

Jang Ilso slowly walked in that direction. Then he quietly squatted in front of the man and lightly pushed his forehead with his index finger.

“Ja... Jang Il...”

The man’s body trembled like a thin tree.

Although nothing was waiting for someone who was already dying, when he made eye contact with Jang Ilso, he was filled with fear, as if he had encountered a demon from hell.

“Scared?”

“Uh... huh...”

“Tsk tsk tsk. Well, it would have been nice if you had listened when I was saying something nice. Then, you wouldn’t have to lose your strength for no reason and could continue to enjoy your glory.”

“...”

“What does it matter what happens to a small clan like this? It would have been just fine if you had come under me and lived happily till the end. Such a moron.”

Bloody tears flowed from the man’s eyes.

It was true that he flatly rejected the Four Precepts Alliance’s offer to give up everything and be their dog. But who would have imagined that just because the offer was refused, such a cruel act would be performed?

Everyone died.

Nothing in this palace, not a single ant, was alive.

The Ten Thousand People Clan’s demons came without notice, cutting, killing, and trampling everyone in this palace. Everyone who had been laughing and talking with him a moment ago had died at the hands of those people.

“Ugh...”

The leader of the Ghost Face Clan, Ghost Face Black Tiger, Mo Song’s face was contorted.

“Why... why so... why...”

“Ahh. I know, I know.”

Jang Ilso smiled.

“I know what you want to say. You want to say that you didn’t intend to put up such a resistance?”

“...”

“If we had come and just threatened, you would have kneeled down at any time. No, no. If we had told you beforehand that we would give you preferential treatment, you would have licked my shoe at any time. I guess that is what you want to say?”

Mo Song’s trembling head nodded slowly.

The sight of the blood-soaked man trembling and nodding his head wasn’t pleasant. But Jang Ilso just smiled brightly.

“You pitiful friend. Think about it. If I listened to every word, I would end up spending my entire time meeting the heads of the Evil Sects. Isn’t that right?”

“...”

“There is a much simpler way than that. I will tell you. So listen carefully.”

Jang Ilso put his mouth to Mo Song’s ear and whispered softly.

“You just crush one suitable place. And the corpses are laid out in a row without being cleaned at all. Then wouldn’t the rumor spread quickly before the body even rots? The clan that rejected the offer of the vicious bastard, Jang Ilso, was massacred without a single rat left behind. The same thing will happen to the other sects that reject the proposal.”

“Uh... Uh...”

Mo Song’s body was visibly shaking.

Jang Ilso lightly patted his shoulder.

“Do you know why I am telling you this?”

“... w... why...?”

A pale smile appeared on Jang Ilso’s beautifully decorated face.

“It’s because I am grateful.”

“...”

“I don’t have any ill feelings towards you because you didn’t do anything particularly wrong. From my perspective, there was just a sect of the right size in just the right place.”

Bloody tears, mixed with hatred, pain, and sadness, flowed from Mo Song’s eyes, but Jang Ilso seemed to feel no emotion.

“Thanks to you, things will be twice as easy. So take it easy. My nature is generous... right. No, that would be a bit difficult. Your corpse has no meaning unless it rots here.”

“Uh... Uhhhhack!”

In the end, Mo Song couldn’t hold back the anger and rushed at Jang Ilso as if he were going to bite him. But, of course, it failed. Jang Ilso calmly grabbed Mo Song’s face as he crawled.

And he tightened his hand so hard that it felt like his skull would break at any moment.

“Ughh... ughhh....”

Mo Song grabbed Jang Ilso’s wrist with his remaining hand. But no matter how hard he tried, Jang Ilso wouldn’t budge and just slowly squeezed his face.

“Oh. You don’t get what I am saying. I tried to send you off in a good way.”

With a smile like a demon, Jang Ilso growled.

“It looks like you still don’t get why you are dying.”

Crack. Crack!

“Ku... ugh....”

The blood in Mo Song’s eyes began to rush out. He struggled and scratched Jang Ilso’s arm, but Jang Ilso didn’t move, as if it didn’t even bother him.

“It is a mortal sin to have no strength and no sense. Even a human being like me lives by paying attention to what everyone thinks, so what does a weak person believe in? If you believed that the world would work as you please for the weak, you would have nothing to say even if you died.”

“Ugh!”

Puak!

In the end, a short groan and a terrible sound rang out, and Mo Song’s body went limp.

“Tsk.”

Jang Ilso got up from the place and shook off his dirty hand.

“Alliance lord.”

Ho Ga-Myung approached him and quietly greeted him.

“The arrangement is done.”

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso glanced around.

“It is still a bit lacking. Let us make more of a mess here and scatter the corpses everywhere.”

“I will.”

“Y-yes.”

Jang Ilso nodded with a gentle face.

“Spread the money elsewhere. I am sorry for surprising them, and tell them that when the Alliance takes over from now, it will be different from the days when they were running rampant.”

“... Is that really needed? Warriors will have no choice but to do what they are told anyway?”

“Tsk tsk tsk. Ga-Myung, you always take the right path, but then you say we aren’t good enough. War is about reducing enemies and increasing allies. There are already too many enemies, so there is no need to create more, even if they are the most insignificant enemies.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand and added quietly.

“If I can make them on my side now for a few pennies, that is a cheap price to pay.”

“I will do as you asked.”

“Y-yes.”

Jang Ilso seemed lost in thought for a moment while looking at the corpses scattered around.

“Aren’t the others moving yet?”

“Yes, it looks like they are taking a wait-and-see approach for now.”

“Those morons.”

The leaders of the Shenzhou Five Sects were probably confused.

“They must be busy thinking their minds out to figure out what position they should ask for in the Alliance. I wonder how far they would push their luck.”

“Isn’t that the nature of humans?”

“Because they are doing such an obvious trick, they are still stuck here, eating dirt.”

Jang Ilso touched his lips. The smell of blood rising from his fingertips lifted the flesh.

‘They are just pigs who are well fed.’

Livestock locked in cages live with only the thought of what they will eat each day. So, they would never come out against them.

To become a wolf, you must not be satisfied with the prey you caught. From the moment the hunt ended, you had to think about the next prey, aim for it, and bite again.

“Alliance lord... this is how we handled the Ghost Face Clan, but even with such warnings, not everyone will follow you.”

“I know.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand dismissively.

“There is no need for everyone to follow. Those who do can be trampled, and those who don’t can be killed.”

“... You mean all those who don’t follow?”

“Why? Is there any problem?”

Ho Ga-Myung fell silent, looking at Jang Ilso, who asked calmly.

Only Jang Ilso would dare to do something like this.

He was the Alliance Lord of the Four Precepts Alliance and the man who tricked the Nine Great Sects into a humiliating treaty. All of the Evil Faction people in the world were paying attention to Jang Ilso.

But even if pushed by his achievements, at least half of the Evil Faction in the Southlands would refuse to join.

But killing all of them?

“Tsk tsk, Ga-Myung. I can see all your thoughts on your face.”

“... I am sorry. There are times when I cannot follow your thoughts.”

“It will happen sooner or later. What is the difference between killing him beforehand or later?”

“...”

“You have to push yourself even if it’s too hard. Every time the Evil Sects form an alliance, the results are always the same. We always get defeated by the Justice Faction.”

“... Right.”

“But right now, the Justice Faction cannot unite.”

Jang Ilso revealed this.

“The fire in the Southlands isn’t important. What is important is that distrust has been planted among them. It means that we blame each other, taunt each other, and end up hating each other. In that situation, you won’t be able to gather under someone’s flag.”

“A-Alliance lord.”

“This is a unique chance. If it weren’t for this one moment, I, too, would become a pig in the Southlands. Before their blood dries and their wounds scab over and heal, they clean up the Southlands and head north. If that happens...”

Crack.

Jang Ilso clenched his fists.

“I will have the world at my feet.”

Ho Ga-Myung trembled and prostrated himself on the spot.

“Of course, it will happen! Hegemony Lord!”

“Tsk! This guy flatters me so much.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand.

“It isn’t that big of a deal. That’s also a story from when things went well. If you deviate even a little, you will just become greedy and become a fool. Then, you, too, will eventually die a pitiful death.”

“It doesn’t matter.”

“Hmm?”

Ho Ga-Myung smiled.

“I will be able to die with a smile because I have stood at the side of the Alliance Lord.”

“...”

Jang Ilso, who was looking at him intently, smiled.

“Strange.”

Jang Ilso turned around and spoke again in a cold voice.

“Get it sorted. I will have to clean up a couple more places tonight.”

“Yes!”

Jang Ilso walked slowly.

‘The die is thrown.’

He also didn’t start with full confidence. This is a test of himself and a challenge to fate.

But he knew one thing for sure.

There were times when you had to organize and move everything, and other times when you had to step into a black fog where you could not see an inch ahead.

What he needed now was the will to take that one step.

‘Nothing can stop me.’

The same went for anything else in the world. A world filled with peace-loving idiots who only cared about their own well-being could not stop him. Never.

“But...”

If there was anything holding him back...

Jang Ilso’s gaze turned south. A distant place that couldn’t be seen from there.

He showed his teeth and grinned eerily.

“But so do you. The Sword Demon of Mount Hua.”

The small stream flowed and became a river, and now it had turned into a rough, turbulent river. No one in the world will be able to stop this flow anymore.

Not even Jang Ilso.

Chapter 841: There Is A Limit To Messing With People! (1)

Over a dozen sects were set on fire in just a few days.

There were countless Evil Sects in the Central Plains. While destruction and fights were common in the world of Evil Sects, where the winners take it all, this was not an ordinary occurrence.

Those who heard the news could not dismiss it lightly.

First, the extinct sects did not just collapse; there were no survivors left.

Second, it was because of Jang Ilso that the sects were destroyed.

Hegemony Lord Jang Ilso, the clan leader of the Ten Thousand People clan and the symbol of the Four Precepts Alliance.

He was a person who immediately united the Evil Sects, which had been at odds with one another for a long time. He brought about a humiliating treaty upon the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, who had dominated Kangho for the past 100 years.

He achieved what no one else dared to dream of turning into reality.

His fame soared to new heights, and the fear his name inspired increased significantly compared to the past.

The fact that Jang Ilso personally took the initiative to exterminate the Evil Sects was telling.

The Unification of the Evil Sects he declared did not end with words. The message that death awaited those who did not agree was clear.

Kangho was burning.

The Evil Sects were forced to make a choice.

Just as Shaolin, Wudang, and Namgung had done before, they had to choose between a humiliating existence or extinction.

Of course, they tried to resist. They attempted to question and understand.

But after witnessing other clans and sects reduced to nothing but ashes, they had no choice but to keep their mouths shut and remain silent.

They also sensed it: the direction of Jang Ilso’s madness was by no means limited to the Justice faction. To him, everything was something to trample and burn.

The Justice Factions also seemed shocked.

If it had been before, they would have shouted and advanced south. Everyone would have said with one voice that a major war could be avoided if they stepped on them before they gathered more strength.

But they couldn’t do it now.

This was because those who were supposed to kill the Evil Sects by standing in the vanguard position couldn’t even set foot in the Southlands.

Even if they wanted to, they now knew their place.

Only when Jang Ilso came to the fore did people realize how much of a role the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families had played. And that reality soon turned to terrible hatred against the Wudang, Shaolin, and Namgung families, who decided something on their own.

It was the same for the Nine Great Sects.

Whenever something like this happened, they always came together and formed a coalition centered around Shaolin, Wudang, and Namgung. It was like that when the Demonic Sect had invaded, and it was as such when more foreign forces stepped into the Central Plains. So, it had to be the same now.

But that couldn’t happen. This was because of the hatred towards those sects, which arbitrarily concluded this non-aggression pact with other sects in order to save their own disciples, and this fact burned strong for the Justice faction.

Because they hated it, they couldn’t join together, and because they couldn’t join, they couldn’t talk. Since they couldn’t talk, they couldn’t unite, and as they didn’t unite, they lacked a solution.

The situation would have been different if Jang Ilso had declared an advance to the north and crossed the Yangtze River. They could have gone, saying that they couldn’t recognize the treaty concluded by the independent sects. If that had happened, then the treaty would have been invalidated.

But Jang Ilso only solidified the land he had gained.

Breaking an unreasonable treaty to protect one’s home might be acceptable, but ignoring a treaty to attack someone else’s land wasn’t the right policy for the Justice Faction.

So unless Jang Ilso crossed into their land, the Justice Faction could only sit idly by and watch as the Gangnam region unified around them.

“Did he plan this from the beginning?”

“That’s ridiculous. How far ahead could he possibly see? If that Evil Faction scoundrel isn’t a member of the Zhuge family, how could he have predicted all this? I suppose the circumstances just fell into place for him.”

“Damn it, if only the Wudang hadn’t done something like that!”

Those who watched the situation helplessly were consumed by anxiety.

The world understood. The power that has been amassed will inevitably be wielded. Nowadays, that force was sweeping across the Southlands, and once the situation was resolved, the power up north would grow.

And yet, they couldn’t prevent the Four Precepts Alliance from sharpening their swords.

In the Southlands, the Evil Faction waged war under the guise of Unification. Along the Yangtze River, many people suffered due to these same scoundrels running rampant.

But those responsible for the entire situation remained silent, with their gates locked.

Only then did everyone realize it.

The fact that the peace of Kangho, which had lasted for over 100 years since the Demonic Sect’s defeat, had come to an end.

The world was entering difficult times.

And in the whirlwind of turbulent times, it wasn’t just the Nine Great Sects and the Evils Sect that captured the world’s attention.

“Damn it...”

Anger filled the faces of those gathered.

Bang!

The person who was drinking slammed the table.

“Can someone live with this anxiety? Should I just sit back while those evil scoundrels run rampant across the river?”

“... calm down. Isn’t this happening all over the land?”

“How can you speak so casually?”

The man couldn’t contain his anger.

“Would those evil scoundrels do something like that just to survive among us? Of course, they will surely come to attack us too!”

“...”

“As soon as they see signs of people gathering here, they will attack! If we just wait and look, we will end up in hell! Damn it...!”

This place was better.

When he thought about the hardships those living in the Southlands would face, he felt hopeless, even though it was someone else’s problem.

“It felt like hell just from seeing all the bastards rampaging around the Yangtze River... now even the Four Precepts Alliance.”

“Ah, about that. These days, the Yangtze River is quiet.”

“Uh? How? Did the pirates leave the Yangtze River?”

“It isn’t that. The Heavenly Friends Alliance on the Yangtze River is cutting down the Evil Sects rampaging around and has secured a commercial route to the Yangtze.”

“What? Commercial route?”

The man’s eyes widened.

It was understandable that the Yangtze was cleared. The Evil Sect running around were more like fools there. If they looked at it, they were no different from the rabble.

The problem was that no one could step forward and suppress them. However securing a path in the Yangtze River occupied by the pirates was the problem.

“Are the pirates staying still?”

“They say they don’t even dare.”

“Is that possible though? Couldn’t that be wrong information?”

“Yah! Rumors are the last things we get from the Yangtze River. The northern side of the Yangtze is gathering at the mouth of the river. They say that the Heavenly Friends Alliance has taken over the land, making it impossible for the Evil Sects to get closer.”

“... The Justice Faction shouldn’t go close to the Yangtze River, right?”

“They have nothing to do with the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families, right?”

This word held a lot of meaning.

Until now, the Justice Faction was represented by the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families. If those who used the name of the Justice faction weren’t part of those 14 sects, even the medium-sized sects in the world were also bound by the name.

But now, the perception was changing.

“The Heavenly Friends Alliance is handling the pirates... no, the Four Precepts Alliance?”

“There is nothing they cannot do! Think about it. The Heavenly Friends Alliance includes the Tang family and Mount Hua. And aren’t the sects outside the wall also part of it?”

“... They won’t be helpful since they are far away, and it’s just two existing sects then?”

“No, from what I heard, the Green Forest isn’t part of the alliance but is helping them.”

“Why would the Green Forest help?”

“Tsk tsk! It’s obvious. Isn’t the Ten Thousand People clan on bad terms with the Green Forest? Since all those people created the Four Sects Alliance, it’s inevitable for the Green Forest to have bad ties with them. So, they should take the side against them!”

A unified faction against the Four Precepts Alliance?

“Was the Heavenly Friends Alliance such a strong force...”

“Didn’t you hear? Mount Hua is the place that fought against the Ten Thousand People the most in the Yangtze River. They say if it wasn’t for Mount Hua, forget the treaty; even the sects would have been wiped out.”

“... Were they that great?”

“If you include the Tang family and Green Forest with Mount Hua, then they are untouchable.”

“No, I think there are too many rumors going around?”

“Tsk, you know nothing. It’s not the Mount Hua of the past. Even though Mount Hua has taken over the river and declared an island there, the pirates haven’t gotten close to it.”

“...”

“When the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families took care of their sects, the Heavenly Friends Alliance stayed on the Yangtze, keeping the pirates in check and providing help and relief. These are the Justice factions!”

But someone whose doubts weren’t resolved tilted his head at those words.

“... No, but that is a bit... what kind of justice faction leads bandits into a fight?”

“Now, now, look at you. You moron! If they hadn’t accepted the help of bandits, could they have saved the civilians on the Yangtze River? I wonder if they could fight against the Evil Sect with righteousness!”

“...”

“These bastards and their reasons! What happened to those who always spoke of being on the right path? Didn’t they abandon the helpless people and save their own lives? And these people, didn’t care about being perceived wrongly and took help to protect people! Now speak! What kind of righteousness do you want?”

“Well, after hearing you, it sounds right.”

“If it wasn’t for the Heavenly Friends Alliance, the Yangtze River would be a hell now. Thanks to them, people can stay in Yangtze.”

“Yes...”

Everyone who listened to the man sighed.

“... I thought all those who called themselves the Justice Factions were the same.”

“Right. I never thought there would be people holding ground in the middle of the dangerous Yangtze.”

At that time, someone tilted their head again.

“But I don’t get it. Is it really the Heavenly Friends Alliance fighting the Precepts Sects Alliance? At best, they are just the small and medium sects.”

“You, don’t ever say that when you go to the Yangtze River.”

“Why?”

“You will die from being beaten.”

The person who gave the advice seriously clicked his tongue.

“For the people of Yangtze now, Mount Hua is the most important thing in the world. And... it doesn’t seem like they are grateful through just emotions alone.”

“What does that mean now?”

“This is news which came from the Southlands.”

“Uh?”

“It seems that the situations are slowly coming out from the Evil Sect bastards who participated in the Yangtze River.”

“Is there anything special about what those bastards speak?”

“Nope, but there is one.”

“What?”

“Do you know who fought against the Hegemony Lord during the war? Do you know who went against Jang Ilso, one one-on-one?”

“Venerable Heo Do... or maybe the Namgung family head.”

“Wrong.”

“Uh? Then maybe Qingcheng?”

“No! Didn’t I tell you it was Mount Hua!”

“Mount Hua? Who in Mount Hua?”

“It is Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon who fought against the Hegemony Lord.”

The person who heard this became angry, thinking it was all nonsense.

“What nonsense are you spouting! Isn’t Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon still a disciple? Is it possible for him to fight against the Hegemony Lord?”

“Who did I say vouched for these words?”

“That...”

The man went silent.

It was clearly from the mouths of the Evil Sects who fought there. The Four Precepts Alliance was a symbol of pride for them. They wouldn’t uselessly tarnish the reputation of their lord.

“These are the words from those who faced the enemy and survived. These are the words of those who crossed the river and settled around. All are saying the same damn thing.”

Those listening gulped and looked at each other. They were curious about what happened next.

“It is said that Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon went against Jang Ilso, and it was an even fight. It is said that the sword of Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon covered the sky, and his courage shook the cliffs. But just as the winner was about to be decided... it was Venerable Heo Do who attacked Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon to prevent the result!”

“T-that... I will tear him to death!”

“Which is why you should stop speaking nonsense. If there is a sect in the world that can go against the newly formed Four Precepts Alliance, it is just one: Mount Hua.”

The man couldn’t refute it and just laughed in disbelief.

“Oh my, Mount Hua... Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon....”

“Ah, right. He is no longer Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.”

“Uh? What does that mean?”

“The title given only to upcoming warriors no longer suits him. A new title has been bestowed upon him. There is even a saying that this title was given to him by the Evil Sect bastards who participated in the war!”

“W-what is that title?”

“That is...”

Chapter 842: There Is A Limit To Messing With People! (2)

“Ahem!”

Even Hyun Jong couldn’t help but cover his mouth, though his shoulders shook with suppressed mirth.

Hyun Jong managed to speak, forcing himself to maintain composure.

“... what... was that?”

Hong Dae-Kwang, perhaps unsure if Hyun Jong hadn’t heard properly or simply wanted to repeat it, answered again.

“So, there is a new title for Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon.”

With all eyes on him, he gestured dramatically, like a representative making an announcement.

“Ugh! The brave martial artist who goes head to head with Jang Ilso! A high-spirited person who never backs down! We praise the spirit of not losing cooperation when everyone else abandons searching their own way...”

At that, everyone’s bodies began to tremble.

“The world calls us exalted; exalt that name!”

The four words felt like nails on the ears of those who were shaking with laughter.

“Mount! Hua! Sword! Saint!”

’Kuak! AUHHH! I am dying! Oh my, this is hella funny!“

“Sword Saint! Huh, Sword Saint!”

“Mount Hua’s Sword Saint! Kuak, right! For sure!”

Chung Myung’s face turned bright red, his fist trembling with irritation.

“This...”

He knew the moment he heard it who had given him the title and spread it around.

“This... this fucking bastard... never stops messing with people!”

Jang Ilso.

It was a name clearly given by that troublemaker!

There was no need to confirm it, as the blatant intention was obvious.

Usually, when praising the swordsmanship of someone from Mount Hua, ‘plum’ was added to the title. Hence, it should be the Plum Blossom Sword Saint, not Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.

It might be because Jang Ilso didn’t know, but if Chung Myung’s technique was to be praised, it should have been Plum Blossom Sword Saint. But instead, Chung Myung’s new title had Mount Hua in it!

The intention was to make it known that Chung Myung belonged to the Mount Hua Sect.

Besides, wasn’t the word attached to it none other than ‘saint’?

In the current situation, there was a clear intention for the name with the word ‘saint’ to spread throughout the world.

To upset him further...

Tak.

At that moment, a hand was placed on Chung Myung’s shoulder.

Jo Gul was kicked by Chung Myung, flew away, and landed on the wall. But even as he writhed in pain, laughter kept coming out of his mouth.

Hyun Jong covered his face with both hands.

It was a normal occurrence, but the intention behind all this was different. Chung Myung looked at Hyun Jong’s shoulders moving.

There was even drool flowing down between his hands too.

But now, the Heavenly Friends Alliance has proven its justification and strength. If we hold the reins a little longer, those who conform to the order of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families will come rushing to knock on the door of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”
